# ============================================================
# STEP 4 — DATA NORMALIZATION AND HELPER FUNCTIONS
# ============================================================
#
# Purpose:
#   Standardize column names, operating-condition metadata,
#   sample identifiers, and condition labels.
#
# IMPORTANT — CALCE SOURCE COLUMN SEMANTICS:
#
#   Excel "Rate"        = charge cutoff current (C/5, C/40)
#   Excel "Charge_Rate" = discharge C-rate (0.7C, 1C, 2C)
#   Excel "Temperature" = nominal temperature (°C)
#
# The original helper function names are retained for
# compatibility with subsequent processing blocks.
#
# Invalid or unrecognized values remain explicitly missing.
# ============================================================

import re
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. NORMALIZE COLUMN NAMES
# ------------------------------------------------------------

def clean_colname(x):
    if x is None or pd.isna(x):
        return ""

    name = str(x).strip().replace("\n", " ")
    name = re.sub(r"\s+", "_", name)
    name = re.sub(r"[^A-Za-z0-9_]+", "", name)

    return name.lower()


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. NORMALIZE DISCHARGE C-RATE
#
# Raw Excel column: Charge_Rate
#
# Expected values:
#   0.7C, 1C, 2C
#
# Returns:
#   float or np.nan
# ------------------------------------------------------------

def normalize_rate(x):
    if x is None or pd.isna(x):
        return np.nan

    text = str(x).strip().upper()
    text = text.replace(" ", "")
    text = text.replace(",", ".")

    if text.endswith("C"):
        text = text[:-1]

    try:
        value = float(text)
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        return np.nan

    if not np.isfinite(value) or value <= 0:
        return np.nan

    return value


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. NORMALIZE TEMPERATURE
#
# Expected nominal values:
#   10, 25, 45, 60 °C
#
# Fractional temperatures are not silently rounded.
#
# Returns:
#   int or np.nan
# ------------------------------------------------------------

def normalize_temp(x):
    if x is None or pd.isna(x):
        return np.nan

    text = str(x).strip().upper()
    text = text.replace(" ", "")
    text = text.replace(",", ".")
    text = text.replace("°C", "")
    text = text.replace("ºC", "")

    if text.endswith("C"):
        text = text[:-1]

    try:
        value = float(text)
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        return np.nan

    if not np.isfinite(value):
        return np.nan

    if not np.isclose(
        value, round(value), atol=1e-9, rtol=0
    ):
        return np.nan

    return int(round(value))


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. NORMALIZE CHARGE CUTOFF CURRENT
#
# Raw Excel column: Rate
#
# Expected values:
#   C/5, C/40
#
# Returns:
#   "C/5", "C/40", or None
#
# Unknown categories are not silently accepted.
# ------------------------------------------------------------

def normalize_charge_rate(x):
    if x is None or pd.isna(x):
        return None

    text = str(x).strip().upper()
    text = text.replace(" ", "")
    text = text.replace(",", ".")
    text = text.replace("\\", "/")
    text = text.replace("–", "-")
    text = text.replace("—", "-")

    aliases = {
        "C/5": "C/5",
        "C5": "C/5",
        "C-5": "C/5",
        "1/5C": "C/5",
        "1/5": "C/5",

        "C/40": "C/40",
        "C40": "C/40",
        "C-40": "C/40",
        "1/40C": "C/40",
        "1/40": "C/40",
    }

    if text in aliases:
        return aliases[text]

    if text.endswith("C"):
        text = text[:-1]

    try:
        value = float(text)
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        return None

    if not np.isfinite(value):
        return None

    if np.isclose(
        value, 0.2, atol=1e-6, rtol=0
    ):
        return "C/5"

    if np.isclose(
        value, 0.025, atol=1e-6, rtol=0
    ):
        return "C/40"

    return None


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 5. CONVERT INTEGRAL NUMERIC VALUES
#
# Intended for:
#   sample numbers and cycle indices
#
# Fractional identifiers are rejected rather than truncated.
# ------------------------------------------------------------

def to_int_or_nan(x):
    if x is None or pd.isna(x):
        return np.nan

    try:
        value = float(
            str(x).strip().replace(",", ".")
        )
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        return np.nan

    if not np.isfinite(value):
        return np.nan

    if not np.isclose(
        value, round(value), atol=1e-9, rtol=0
    ):
        return np.nan

    return int(round(value))


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 6. BUILD CANONICAL CONDITION KEY
#
# Canonical order:
#   (cutoff current, temperature, discharge C-rate)
#
# The first argument retains its legacy name for
# compatibility with existing processing code.
# ------------------------------------------------------------

def condition_key(charge_rate, temp, discharge_rate):
    cutoff = normalize_charge_rate(charge_rate)
    temperature = normalize_temp(temp)
    discharge = normalize_rate(discharge_rate)

    if np.isfinite(discharge):
        discharge = round(discharge, 3)
    else:
        discharge = np.nan

    return cutoff, temperature, discharge


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 7. GENERATE READABLE CONDITION LABEL
# ------------------------------------------------------------

def condition_label(cond):
    cutoff, temperature, discharge = cond

    cutoff_text = (
        cutoff if cutoff is not None else "NA"
    )

    temp_text = (
        f"{int(temperature)}°C"
        if pd.notna(temperature)
        else "NA"
    )

    discharge_text = (
        f"{float(discharge):g}C"
        if pd.notna(discharge)
        else "NA"
    )

    return (
        f"{cutoff_text} & "
        f"{temp_text} & "
        f"{discharge_text}"
    )


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 8. BASIC NORMALIZATION CHECKS
# ------------------------------------------------------------

assert normalize_charge_rate("C/5") == "C/5"
assert normalize_charge_rate("0.025") == "C/40"
assert normalize_rate("0.7C") == 0.7
assert normalize_rate("2C") == 2.0
assert normalize_temp("25 °C") == 25
assert to_int_or_nan("108.0") == 108
assert np.isnan(to_int_or_nan(108.5))

assert condition_key(
    "C/40", 25, "2C"
) == ("C/40", 25, 2.0)

print("=" * 70)
print("DATA NORMALIZATION HELPERS READY")
print("=" * 70)
print("Normalization checks passed.")
print("Canonical condition order: cutoff, temperature, discharge.")
print("=" * 70)

# ============================================================
# STEP 5 — EXPECTED CONDITION MAPS AND DESIGN VALIDATION
# ============================================================
#
# Purpose:
#   Construct and validate the expected CALCE experimental
#   condition maps using the documented sample numbering.
#
# Experimental design:
#   Temperatures:          10, 25, 45, 60 °C
#   Charge cutoff currents: C/5, C/40
#   Discharge rates:       0.7C, 1C, 2C
#
# Total operating conditions:
#   4 temperatures x 2 cutoff currents x 3 discharge rates
#   = 24 conditions
#
# Reference maps:
#   1. Full theoretical design: 192 sample positions
#   2. Documented design:       182 listed sample positions
#
# Documented exceptions:
#   Condition 23: samples 177, 178, 179
#   Condition 24: samples 185, 186, 187
#
# The expected maps are reference metadata.
# Actual sample availability is checked after data loading.
#
# ============================================================

import pandas as pd


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. EXPERIMENTAL DESIGN
# ------------------------------------------------------------

EXPECTED_TEMPERATURES = (10, 25, 45, 60)

EXPECTED_DISCHARGE_RATES = (0.7, 1.0, 2.0)

EXPECTED_CUTOFF_RATES = ("C/5", "C/40")

EXPECTED_CONDITIONS = 24

EXPECTED_FULL_SAMPLES = 192

EXPECTED_DOCUMENTED_SAMPLES = 182


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. DOCUMENTED PARTIAL CONDITIONS
# ------------------------------------------------------------

DOCUMENTED_PARTIAL_SAMPLES = {
    23: (177, 178, 179),
    24: (185, 186, 187),
}


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. BUILD EXPECTED CONDITION TABLE
# ------------------------------------------------------------

def build_condition_table(full_192=True):
    """
    Build the expected sample-to-condition mapping.

    Parameters
    ----------
    full_192 : bool
        True:
            Return the complete theoretical 192-position map.

        False:
            Return the 182 sample positions documented
            in the CALCE dataset description.

    Returns
    -------
    pandas.DataFrame
        One row per expected sample position.

    Notes
    -----
    Column names are retained for compatibility with
    the original dataset consistency-check code.

    Expected_Charge_Rate represents the charge cutoff
    current (C/5 or C/40).
    """

    rows = []
    condition_id = 0

    for temp in EXPECTED_TEMPERATURES:

        for discharge in EXPECTED_DISCHARGE_RATES:

            for cutoff in EXPECTED_CUTOFF_RATES:

                condition_id += 1

                start_sample = (
                    (condition_id - 1) * 8 + 1
                )

                theoretical_samples = tuple(
                    range(
                        start_sample,
                        start_sample + 8
                    )
                )

                if full_192:
                    samples = theoretical_samples

                else:
                    samples = DOCUMENTED_PARTIAL_SAMPLES.get(
                        condition_id,
                        theoretical_samples
                    )

                condition = (
                    cutoff,
                    temp,
                    discharge
                )

                label = condition_label(condition)

                for sample_id in samples:

                    rows.append({
                        "Condition": condition_id,
                        "sample_number": int(sample_id),
                        "Expected_Charge_Rate": cutoff,
                        "Expected_Temperature": int(temp),
                        "Expected_Discharge_Rate": float(
                            discharge
                        ),
                        "Expected_Label": label,
                    })

    result = pd.DataFrame(rows)

    return result


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. GENERATE BOTH REFERENCE TABLES
# ------------------------------------------------------------

expected_full = build_condition_table(
    full_192=True
)

expected_docx = build_condition_table(
    full_192=False
)


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 5. CONSTRUCT SAMPLE ID SETS
# ------------------------------------------------------------

full_sample_set = set(
    expected_full["sample_number"].astype(int)
)

docx_sample_set = set(
    expected_docx["sample_number"].astype(int)
)


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 6. BUILD LOOKUP DICTIONARIES
# ------------------------------------------------------------

def build_expected_map(table):
    """
    Return a sample-indexed reference mapping.

    Dictionary value:
        (
            Condition_ID,
            charge cutoff current,
            temperature,
            discharge C-rate,
            readable condition label
        )
    """

    mapping = {}

    for row in table.itertuples(index=False):

        sample_id = int(row.sample_number)

        if sample_id in mapping:
            raise ValueError(
                f"Duplicate sample number: {sample_id}"
            )

        mapping[sample_id] = (
            int(row.Condition),
            str(row.Expected_Charge_Rate),
            int(row.Expected_Temperature),
            round(
                float(row.Expected_Discharge_Rate),
                3
            ),
            str(row.Expected_Label),
        )

    return mapping


expected_full_map = build_expected_map(
    expected_full
)

expected_docx_map = build_expected_map(
    expected_docx
)


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 7. STRUCTURAL VALIDATION
# ------------------------------------------------------------

assert len(expected_full) == EXPECTED_FULL_SAMPLES, (
    "The theoretical map must contain 192 rows."
)

assert len(expected_docx) == EXPECTED_DOCUMENTED_SAMPLES, (
    "The documented map must contain 182 rows."
)

assert expected_full["Condition"].nunique() == (
    EXPECTED_CONDITIONS
)

assert expected_docx["Condition"].nunique() == (
    EXPECTED_CONDITIONS
)

assert len(full_sample_set) == EXPECTED_FULL_SAMPLES

assert len(docx_sample_set) == EXPECTED_DOCUMENTED_SAMPLES

assert set(expected_full["Condition"]) == set(
    range(1, EXPECTED_CONDITIONS + 1)
)

assert set(expected_docx["Condition"]) == set(
    range(1, EXPECTED_CONDITIONS + 1)
)

assert docx_sample_set.issubset(full_sample_set)


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 8. VERIFY DOCUMENTED PARTIAL CONDITIONS
# ------------------------------------------------------------

for condition_id, expected_samples in (
    DOCUMENTED_PARTIAL_SAMPLES.items()
):

    actual_samples = tuple(
        expected_docx.loc[
            expected_docx["Condition"] == condition_id,
            "sample_number"
        ].astype(int)
    )

    assert actual_samples == expected_samples, (
        f"Condition {condition_id}: "
        "documented sample mapping mismatch."
    )


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 9. VERIFY CONDITION COUNTS
# ------------------------------------------------------------

full_counts = (
    expected_full
    .groupby("Condition")
    .size()
)

docx_counts = (
    expected_docx
    .groupby("Condition")
    .size()
)

assert (full_counts == 8).all()

for condition_id in range(1, 25):

    expected_count = (
        3 if condition_id in DOCUMENTED_PARTIAL_SAMPLES
        else 8
    )

    assert int(docx_counts.loc[condition_id]) == (
        expected_count
    )


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 10. IDENTIFY THEORETICAL BUT UNDOCUMENTED IDS
# ------------------------------------------------------------

undocumented_sample_ids = sorted(
    full_sample_set - docx_sample_set
)

assert undocumented_sample_ids == (
    list(range(180, 185))
    +
    list(range(188, 193))
)


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 11. PRINT VALIDATION SUMMARY
# ------------------------------------------------------------

print("=" * 70)
print("EXPECTED CONDITION MAPS — VALIDATION")
print("=" * 70)

print(
    "Experimental conditions:",
    expected_docx["Condition"].nunique()
)

print(
    "Theoretical sample positions:",
    len(expected_full)
)

print(
    "Documented sample positions:",
    len(expected_docx)
)

print(
    "Theoretical positions absent from the document:",
    len(undocumented_sample_ids)
)

print(
    "\nCondition 23 documented samples:",
    list(DOCUMENTED_PARTIAL_SAMPLES[23])
)

print(
    "Condition 24 documented samples:",
    list(DOCUMENTED_PARTIAL_SAMPLES[24])
)

print(
    "\nUndocumented theoretical sample IDs:",
    undocumented_sample_ids
)

print("\nDocumented sample counts by condition:")

print(
    docx_counts.to_string()
)

print("\n" + "=" * 70)
print("EXPECTED CONDITION MAP VALIDATION PASSED")
print("=" * 70)

print(
    "Reference metadata are ready for comparison "
    "with observed Excel records."
)

# ============================================================
# STEP 6 — COLUMN DETECTION AND SOURCE SCHEMA VALIDATION
# ============================================================
#
# Purpose:
#   Identify required CALCE dataset columns using normalized
#   header names and controlled aliases.
#
# IMPORTANT — ORIGINAL CALCE COLUMN SEMANTICS:
#
#   Rate        = charge cutoff current (C/5, C/40)
#   Charge_Rate = discharge C-rate (0.7C, 1C, 2C)
#
# Canonical keys retain their original source names for
# compatibility with the subsequent processing blocks.
#
# Ambiguous column matches are rejected explicitly.
# ============================================================


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. ACCEPTED COLUMN ALIASES
# ------------------------------------------------------------

POSSIBLE_COLUMNS = {

    "sample_number": [
        "sample_number",
        "samplenumber",
        "sample_no",
        "sampleno",
        "sample",
        "sample_id",
        "sample_int",
        "cell_id",
        "cell_no",
        "cellnumber",
        "cell",
    ],

    # ORIGINAL SOURCE: Rate = charge cutoff current
    "rate": [
        "rate",
        "charge_cutoff",
        "charge_cut_off",
        "charge_cutoff_current",
        "charge_cut_off_current",
        "cutoff_current",
        "cut_off_current",
    ],

    "temperature": [
        "temperature",
        "temp",
        "ambient_temperature",
        "ambienttemperature",
    ],

    # ORIGINAL SOURCE: Charge_Rate = discharge C-rate
    "charge_rate": [
        "charge_rate",
        "chargerate",
        "discharge_rate",
        "dischargerate",
        "discharge_crate",
        "discharge_c_rate",
        "c_rate",
        "crate",
    ],

    "cycle_index": [
        "cycle_index",
        "cycleindex",
        "cycle_number",
        "cyclenumber",
        "cycle_no",
        "cycleno",
        "cycle",
    ],

    "soc": [
        "soc",
        "stateofcharge",
        "state_of_charge",
    ],

    "voltage": [
        "voltagev",
        "voltage_v",
        "voltage",
    ],

    "current": [
        "currenta",
        "current_a",
        "current",
    ],
}


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. VALIDATE ALIAS DEFINITIONS
# ------------------------------------------------------------

alias_owners = {}

for canonical, aliases in POSSIBLE_COLUMNS.items():

    for alias in aliases:

        cleaned_alias = clean_colname(alias)

        if cleaned_alias in alias_owners:

            if alias_owners[cleaned_alias] != canonical:

                raise ValueError(
                    "Column alias conflict: "
                    f"'{cleaned_alias}' belongs to both "
                    f"'{alias_owners[cleaned_alias]}' "
                    f"and '{canonical}'."
                )

        alias_owners[cleaned_alias] = canonical


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. DETECT COLUMNS
# ------------------------------------------------------------

def detect_columns(header):
    """
    Detect CALCE dataset columns.

    Parameters
    ----------
    header : sequence
        Column names read from an Excel worksheet.

    Returns
    -------
    detected : dict
        Canonical column names mapped to zero-based indices.
        Missing columns are represented by None.

    cleaned : list
        Normalized column names in their original order.

    Raises
    ------
    ValueError
        If multiple columns match the same canonical field.

    Notes
    -----
    Source metadata semantics:

        rate        -> charge cutoff current
        charge_rate -> discharge C-rate
    """

    cleaned = [
        clean_colname(column)
        for column in header
    ]

    detected = {}

    for canonical, candidates in POSSIBLE_COLUMNS.items():

        accepted = set(candidates)

        matches = [
            idx
            for idx, column_name in enumerate(cleaned)
            if column_name in accepted
        ]

        if len(matches) > 1:

            conflicting_headers = [
                str(header[idx])
                for idx in matches
            ]

            raise ValueError(
                f"Ambiguous columns for '{canonical}': "
                + ", ".join(conflicting_headers)
            )

        detected[canonical] = (
            matches[0]
            if matches
            else None
        )

    return detected, cleaned


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. REQUIRED COLUMN DEFINITIONS
# ------------------------------------------------------------

REQUIRED_COLUMNS = [
    "sample_number",
    "rate",
    "temperature",
    "charge_rate",
    "cycle_index",
]

OPTIONAL_COLUMNS = [
    "soc",
    "voltage",
    "current",
]


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 5. VALIDATE DETECTED SOURCE SCHEMA
# ------------------------------------------------------------

def validate_detected_columns(detected, header, file_name):
    """
    Check required columns and report detected source fields.
    """

    missing = [
        column
        for column in REQUIRED_COLUMNS
        if detected.get(column) is None
    ]

    if missing:

        raise ValueError(
            f"Missing required columns in {file_name}: "
            + ", ".join(missing)
            + "\nAvailable columns: "
            + ", ".join(str(x) for x in header)
        )

    return True


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 6. BASIC COLUMN DETECTION TESTS
# ------------------------------------------------------------

test_header = [
    "Sample_Number",
    "Rate",
    "Temperature",
    "Charge_Rate",
    "Cycle_Index",
    "SoC",
    "Voltage(V)",
    "Current(A)",
]

test_detected, test_cleaned = detect_columns(
    test_header
)

assert validate_detected_columns(
    test_detected,
    test_header,
    "test_dataset.xlsx"
)

assert test_detected["sample_number"] == 0
assert test_detected["rate"] == 1
assert test_detected["temperature"] == 2
assert test_detected["charge_rate"] == 3
assert test_detected["cycle_index"] == 4
assert test_detected["soc"] == 5
assert test_detected["voltage"] == 6
assert test_detected["current"] == 7

# Verify alternative column aliases.
alias_header = [
    "Cell_ID",
    "Charge_Cutoff",
    "Temp",
    "Discharge_Rate",
    "Cycle_Number",
]

alias_detected, _ = detect_columns(
    alias_header
)

assert all(
    alias_detected[column] is not None
    for column in REQUIRED_COLUMNS
)

# Verify that ambiguous matches are rejected.
try:

    detect_columns([
        "Sample_Number",
        "Rate",
        "Charge_Cutoff",
        "Temperature",
        "Charge_Rate",
        "Cycle_Index",
    ])

except ValueError:
    pass

else:
    raise AssertionError(
        "Ambiguous column detection test failed."
    )


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 7. PRINT VALIDATION SUMMARY
# ------------------------------------------------------------

print("=" * 70)
print("CALCE COLUMN DETECTION — VALIDATION")
print("=" * 70)

for canonical, index in test_detected.items():

    source_name = (
        test_header[index]
        if index is not None
        else "NOT FOUND"
    )

    print(
        f"{canonical:<18} -> {source_name}"
    )

print("\n" + "=" * 70)
print("COLUMN DETECTION VALIDATION PASSED")
print("=" * 70)

print("Required source fields: 5")
print("Optional source fields: 3")
print("Ambiguous column detection: enabled")
print("Metadata mappings: verified")

# ============================================================
# STEP 7 — MEMORY-EFFICIENT EXCEL DATA EXTRACTION AND QA
# ============================================================
# Reads the three processed CALCE workbooks without loading
# complete worksheets into pandas DataFrames.
#
# Verified metadata semantics:
#   Rate        -> charge cutoff current (C/5 or C/40)
#   Charge_Rate -> discharge rate (0.7C, 1C or 2C)
#
# Retains the original summary variables used in STEP 8.
# No filtering of valid observed sample IDs is performed.
# ============================================================

file_summaries = []
overall_samples = set()
overall_conditions = Counter()
overall_sample_conditions = defaultdict(Counter)
overall_sample_files = defaultdict(set)
overall_sample_cycles = defaultdict(set)
overall_cycle_minmax = defaultdict(lambda: [np.inf, -np.inf])
overall_soc_minmax = defaultdict(lambda: [np.inf, -np.inf])

rows_by_file_condition = Counter()
rows_by_file_sample = Counter()

required_cols = [
    "sample_number", "rate", "temperature",
    "charge_rate", "cycle_index",
]

# Small diagnostic samples; complete counts are retained separately.
issue_examples = defaultdict(list)


def record_issue(file_path, row_number, issue, details):
    """Retain at most three examples per file and issue category."""
    key = (str(file_path), issue)
    if len(issue_examples[key]) < 3:
        issue_examples[key].append(
            {"excel_row": row_number, "details": str(details)[:160]}
        )


def parse_finite_soc(value):
    """Read an observed SoC value without changing its scale."""
    if value is None or (isinstance(value, str) and not value.strip()):
        return np.nan
    try:
        result = float(str(value).strip().replace(",", "."))
    except (ValueError, TypeError, OverflowError):
        return np.nan
    return result if np.isfinite(result) else np.nan


for file_name in xlsx_files:
    print(f"\nReading: {file_name}")
    wb = load_workbook(str(file_name), read_only=True, data_only=True)

    try:
        sheet_name = (
            "Step5_Data" if "Step5_Data" in wb.sheetnames
            else wb.sheetnames[0]
        )
        ws = wb[sheet_name]
        if sheet_name != "Step5_Data":
            print(f"[NOTICE] Step5_Data not found; using sheet: {sheet_name}")

        header_row = next(
            ws.iter_rows(min_row=1, max_row=1, values_only=True),
            None,
        )
        if not header_row or all(value is None for value in header_row):
            raise ValueError(f"Missing Excel header: {file_name}")

        header = list(header_row)
        detected, cleaned_header = detect_columns(header)
        validate_detected_columns(detected, header, str(file_name))

        idx_sample = detected["sample_number"]
        idx_rate = detected["rate"]
        idx_temp = detected["temperature"]
        idx_charge = detected["charge_rate"]
        idx_cycle = detected["cycle_index"]
        idx_soc = detected.get("soc")

        file_samples = set()
        file_conditions = Counter()
        file_cycle_min = np.inf
        file_cycle_max = -np.inf
        counts = Counter()

        for excel_row, row in enumerate(
            ws.iter_rows(min_row=2, values_only=True), start=2
        ):
            counts["n_rows"] += 1

            if all(
                value is None or (isinstance(value, str) and not value.strip())
                for value in row
            ):
                counts["blank_rows"] += 1
                continue

            sample = to_int_or_nan(row[idx_sample])
            if pd.isna(sample) or int(sample) <= 0:
                counts["invalid_sample_rows"] += 1
                record_issue(file_name, excel_row, "invalid_sample", row[idx_sample])
                continue

            sample = int(sample)
            counts["valid_sample_rows"] += 1
            file_samples.add(sample)
            overall_samples.add(sample)
            overall_sample_files[sample].add(str(file_name))
            rows_by_file_sample[(str(file_name), sample)] += 1

            cycle = to_int_or_nan(row[idx_cycle])
            if pd.notna(cycle):
                cycle = int(cycle)
                overall_sample_cycles[sample].add(cycle)
                bounds = overall_cycle_minmax[sample]
                bounds[0] = min(bounds[0], cycle)
                bounds[1] = max(bounds[1], cycle)
                file_cycle_min = min(file_cycle_min, cycle)
                file_cycle_max = max(file_cycle_max, cycle)
                counts["valid_cycle_rows"] += 1
            else:
                counts["invalid_cycle_rows"] += 1
                record_issue(file_name, excel_row, "invalid_cycle", row[idx_cycle])

            if idx_soc is not None:
                soc = parse_finite_soc(row[idx_soc])
                if np.isfinite(soc):
                    bounds = overall_soc_minmax[sample]
                    bounds[0] = min(bounds[0], soc)
                    bounds[1] = max(bounds[1], soc)
                else:
                    counts["missing_or_invalid_soc_rows"] += 1

            # Source 'Rate' is cutoff; source 'Charge_Rate' is discharge.
            cutoff = normalize_charge_rate(row[idx_rate])
            temp = normalize_temp(row[idx_temp])
            discharge = normalize_rate(row[idx_charge])

            if cutoff is None or pd.isna(temp) or pd.isna(discharge):
                counts["invalid_metadata_rows"] += 1
                record_issue(
                    file_name, excel_row, "invalid_metadata",
                    f"Rate={row[idx_rate]!r}; "
                    f"Temperature={row[idx_temp]!r}; "
                    f"Charge_Rate={row[idx_charge]!r}"
                )
                continue

            cond = condition_key(cutoff, temp, discharge)
            if (
                cond[0] not in EXPECTED_CUTOFF_RATES
                or cond[1] not in EXPECTED_TEMPERATURES
                or cond[2] not in EXPECTED_DISCHARGE_RATES
            ):
                counts["unexpected_condition_rows"] += 1
                record_issue(file_name, excel_row, "unexpected_condition", cond)

            file_conditions[cond] += 1
            overall_conditions[cond] += 1
            overall_sample_conditions[sample][cond] += 1
            rows_by_file_condition[(str(file_name), cond)] += 1
            counts["valid_metadata_rows"] += 1

        file_summaries.append({
            "file": str(file_name),
            "sheet_used": sheet_name,
            "n_rows": counts["n_rows"],
            "n_columns": len(header),
            "columns": ", ".join(str(c) for c in header),
            "n_unique_samples": len(file_samples),
            "min_sample": min(file_samples) if file_samples else None,
            "max_sample": max(file_samples) if file_samples else None,
            "n_unique_conditions": len(file_conditions),
            "cycle_min": None if file_cycle_min == np.inf else int(file_cycle_min),
            "cycle_max": None if file_cycle_max == -np.inf else int(file_cycle_max),
            "blank_rows": counts["blank_rows"],
            "valid_sample_rows": counts["valid_sample_rows"],
            "invalid_sample_rows": counts["invalid_sample_rows"],
            "valid_metadata_rows": counts["valid_metadata_rows"],
            "invalid_metadata_rows": counts["invalid_metadata_rows"],
            "unexpected_condition_rows": counts["unexpected_condition_rows"],
            "valid_cycle_rows": counts["valid_cycle_rows"],
            "invalid_cycle_rows": counts["invalid_cycle_rows"],
            "missing_or_invalid_soc_rows": counts["missing_or_invalid_soc_rows"],
        })
    finally:
        wb.close()

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# REPORT: preserve all objects for subsequent QA steps.
# ------------------------------------------------------------
step7_summary = pd.DataFrame(file_summaries)
print("\n" + "=" * 72)
print("STEP 7 — EXCEL EXTRACTION AND STRUCTURAL QA")
print("=" * 72)
print(step7_summary.drop(columns=["columns"]).to_string(index=False))
print(f"\nObserved unique physical sample IDs: {len(overall_samples)}")
print(f"Observed normalized condition keys: {len(overall_conditions)}")
print(f"Observed sample-condition pairs: {sum(len(c) for c in overall_sample_conditions.values())}")
print(f"Observed samples absent from documented map: {len(overall_samples - docx_sample_set)}")
print(f"Documented sample IDs absent from observed data: {len(docx_sample_set - overall_samples)}")

for file_path, category in sorted(issue_examples):
    print(f"\n[QA EXAMPLES] {file_path} — {category}")
    for item in issue_examples[(file_path, category)]:
        print(f"  Excel row {item['excel_row']}: {item['details']}")

if int(step7_summary["invalid_metadata_rows"].sum()) > 0:
    print("\n[REVIEW] Some rows have invalid condition metadata. They were")
    print("         excluded from condition counters, but sample/cycle IDs were retained.")
if int(step7_summary["unexpected_condition_rows"].sum()) > 0:
    print("\n[REVIEW] Unexpected but parseable operating conditions were observed.")
print("=" * 72)
print("STEP 7 READ COMPLETE — REVIEW QA BEFORE ACCEPTING THE DATASET")
print("=" * 72)

# ============================================================
# STEP 9 — DATASET CONSISTENCY: AUTOMATIC CONCLUSION
# ============================================================
# Run after STEP 7 (and preferably after STEP 8 reconciliation).
# Uses previously computed aggregates; does not reopen Excel files.
# All statuses are observational: no source rows are removed.
# ============================================================

from pathlib import Path
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. REUSE THE VERIFIED DATA STRUCTURES
# ------------------------------------------------------------
actual_sample_set = set(overall_samples)
actual_samples = sorted(actual_sample_set)
file_summary_df = step7_summary.copy()

n_samples = len(actual_sample_set)
n_conditions = len(overall_conditions)
n_sample_condition_pairs = sum(
    len(counter) for counter in overall_sample_conditions.values()
)
n_multicondition_samples = sum(
    len(counter) > 1 for counter in overall_sample_conditions.values()
)

if n_samples == 0:
    raise ValueError("No valid sample identifiers were observed in STEP 7.")

unassigned_sample_ids = sorted(
    actual_sample_set - set(overall_sample_conditions)
)

source_condition_rows = sum(overall_conditions.values())
sample_condition_rows = sum(
    sum(counter.values()) for counter in overall_sample_conditions.values()
)
if source_condition_rows != sample_condition_rows:
    raise AssertionError(
        "Condition-level and sample-level row counts do not agree."
    )

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. SAMPLE-SET RECONCILIATION
# ------------------------------------------------------------
missing_vs_full = sorted(full_sample_set - actual_sample_set)
extra_vs_full = sorted(actual_sample_set - full_sample_set)
missing_vs_docx = sorted(docx_sample_set - actual_sample_set)
extra_vs_docx = sorted(actual_sample_set - docx_sample_set)

matches_full_sample_set = actual_sample_set == full_sample_set
matches_docx_sample_set = actual_sample_set == docx_sample_set


def expected_condition_key(mapping_entry):
    """Extract (cutoff, temperature, discharge rate) from STEP 5 mapping."""
    return (mapping_entry[1], mapping_entry[2], mapping_entry[3])


def all_observed_conditions_match(reference_map):
    """Each sample must occur in exactly its single expected condition."""
    return all(
        sample_id in reference_map
        and len(overall_sample_conditions[sample_id]) == 1
        and next(iter(overall_sample_conditions[sample_id]))
            == expected_condition_key(reference_map[sample_id])
        for sample_id in actual_sample_set
    )


all_samples_match_full_conditions = all_observed_conditions_match(
    expected_full_map
)
all_samples_match_docx_conditions = all_observed_conditions_match(
    expected_docx_map
)

# Count pair-level departures from the documented sample-to-condition map.
condition_mismatch_pairs = []
undocumented_pairs = []
for sample_id, counter in sorted(overall_sample_conditions.items()):
    expected = expected_docx_map.get(sample_id)
    for condition, n_rows in counter.items():
        if expected is None:
            undocumented_pairs.append((sample_id, condition, int(n_rows)))
        elif condition != expected_condition_key(expected):
            condition_mismatch_pairs.append((
                sample_id, condition, int(n_rows)
            ))

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. CYCLE-INDEX COVERAGE
# ------------------------------------------------------------
cycle_lows = pd.to_numeric(
    file_summary_df["cycle_min"], errors="coerce"
).dropna()
cycle_highs = pd.to_numeric(
    file_summary_df["cycle_max"], errors="coerce"
).dropna()
min_cycle = int(cycle_lows.min()) if not cycle_lows.empty else None
max_cycle = int(cycle_highs.max()) if not cycle_highs.empty else None

if max_cycle is None:
    cycle_interpretation = (
        "No valid Cycle_Index values were observed; review the source data."
    )
elif max_cycle > 300:
    cycle_interpretation = (
        f"Observed Cycle_Index values reach {max_cycle} (>300). "
        "The Excel records are not limited to indices 1–300. "
        "This does not establish the number of reconstructed valid cycles."
    )
else:
    cycle_interpretation = (
        f"The maximum observed Cycle_Index is {max_cycle} (<=300). "
        "This alone does not establish why the available index range ends there."
    )

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. CLASSIFY THE OBSERVED DATASET WITHOUT FORCING A MATCH
# ------------------------------------------------------------
n_invalid_metadata = int(
    file_summary_df["invalid_metadata_rows"].sum()
)
n_unexpected_conditions = int(
    file_summary_df["unexpected_condition_rows"].sum()
)
schema_ok = (
    n_invalid_metadata == 0
    and n_unexpected_conditions == 0
    and n_conditions == 24
)

if (
    schema_ok and matches_full_sample_set
    and all_samples_match_full_conditions
):
    dataset_status = "FULL_192_MATCH"
    dataset_interpretation = (
        "The observed sample set and unique condition assignments "
        "match the full 192-position reference map."
    )
elif (
    schema_ok and matches_docx_sample_set
    and all_samples_match_docx_conditions
):
    dataset_status = "DOCX_REDUCED_182_MATCH"
    dataset_interpretation = (
        "The observed sample set and unique condition assignments "
        "match the documented 182-position reference map."
    )
elif (
    schema_ok and actual_sample_set.issubset(docx_sample_set)
    and all_samples_match_docx_conditions
):
    dataset_status = "DOCUMENTED_SUBSET_CONDITION_CONSISTENT"
    dataset_interpretation = (
        "All observed sample IDs follow their documented conditions, "
        "but some documented sample IDs were not observed."
    )
else:
    dataset_status = "SAMPLE_CONDITION_MAPPING_REVIEW_REQUIRED"
    dataset_interpretation = (
        "The observed sample set and/or condition assignments differ "
        "from the reference maps. Review missing and extra sample IDs, "
        "multiple condition assignments, and pair-level mismatches "
        "before accepting the dataset."
    )

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 5. EXPORT A HUMAN-READABLE CONCLUSION TABLE
# ------------------------------------------------------------
def id_list(values):
    return ", ".join(map(str, values)) if values else "None"


metrics = [
    ("Total uploaded Excel files", len(xlsx_files)),
    ("Total rows across files", int(file_summary_df["n_rows"].sum())),
    ("Observed unique sample IDs", n_samples),
    ("Observed normalized conditions", n_conditions),
    ("Observed sample-condition pairs", n_sample_condition_pairs),
    ("Sample IDs assigned to multiple conditions", n_multicondition_samples),
    ("Sample IDs without valid condition assignments", id_list(unassigned_sample_ids)),
    ("Condition-mismatched pairs vs DOCX", len(condition_mismatch_pairs)),
    ("Pairs with undocumented sample IDs", len(undocumented_pairs)),
    ("Invalid metadata rows", n_invalid_metadata),
    ("Unexpected-condition rows", n_unexpected_conditions),
    ("Minimum observed sample ID", min(actual_samples)),
    ("Maximum observed sample ID", max(actual_samples)),
    ("Matches full 192 sample set", matches_full_sample_set),
    ("Matches documented 182 sample set", matches_docx_sample_set),
    ("All assignments match full reference", all_samples_match_full_conditions),
    ("All assignments match DOCX reference", all_samples_match_docx_conditions),
    ("Missing sample IDs vs full 192", id_list(missing_vs_full)),
    ("Extra sample IDs vs full 192", id_list(extra_vs_full)),
    ("Missing sample IDs vs DOCX", id_list(missing_vs_docx)),
    ("Extra sample IDs vs DOCX", id_list(extra_vs_docx)),
    ("Minimum observed Cycle_Index", min_cycle),
    ("Maximum observed Cycle_Index", max_cycle),
    ("Dataset status", dataset_status),
    ("Dataset interpretation", dataset_interpretation),
    ("Cycle interpretation", cycle_interpretation),
]
conclusion_df = pd.DataFrame(metrics, columns=["Metric", "Value"])

qa_dir = Path(OUTPUT_DIR) / "dataset_qa"
qa_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
conclusion_path = qa_dir / "dataset_automatic_conclusion.csv"
conclusion_df.to_csv(conclusion_path, index=False, encoding="utf-8-sig")

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 6. DISPLAY THE DECISION AND LIMITATIONS
# ------------------------------------------------------------
print("=" * 76)
print("STEP 9 — DATASET CONSISTENCY: AUTOMATIC CONCLUSION")
print("=" * 76)
print(f"Source rows: {int(file_summary_df['n_rows'].sum()):,}")
print(f"Observed sample IDs: {n_samples}")
print(f"Observed condition keys: {n_conditions}")
print(f"Observed sample-condition pairs: {n_sample_condition_pairs}")
print(f"Sample IDs associated with multiple conditions: {n_multicondition_samples}")
print(f"Samples without valid condition assignments: {id_list(unassigned_sample_ids)}")
print(f"Missing documented sample IDs: {id_list(missing_vs_docx)}")
print(f"Undocumented observed sample IDs: {id_list(extra_vs_docx)}")
print(f"Condition-mismatched pairs: {len(condition_mismatch_pairs)}")
print(f"Pairs under undocumented sample IDs: {len(undocumented_pairs)}")
print(f"Observed Cycle_Index range: {min_cycle} to {max_cycle}")
print(f"\nDATASET STATUS: {dataset_status}")
print(f"INTERPRETATION: {dataset_interpretation}")
print(f"CYCLE COVERAGE: {cycle_interpretation}")
print(f"\nSaved conclusion: {conclusion_path}")
print("No input records were modified or excluded by this conclusion step.")
print("=" * 76)

# ============================================================
# STEP 10 — FINAL DATASET QA REPORT (DISPLAY + EXCEL EXPORT)
# ============================================================
# Run after STEPS 5, 7, and 9 in the same Colab session.
# STEP 8 is optional: its reconciliation tables are rebuilt from
# STEP 7 counters when missing.
# Uses only the existing in-memory QA summaries and counters.
# Does not reopen, change, filter, or reconstruct source records.
# This is an audit report, not an analysis-data selection step.
# ============================================================

from pathlib import Path
from itertools import combinations
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

try:
    from IPython.display import display
except ImportError:
    display = print

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. CHECK REQUIRED UPSTREAM OBJECTS
# ------------------------------------------------------------
required_objects = [
    'OUTPUT_DIR', 'xlsx_files', 'expected_full', 'expected_docx',
    'expected_full_map', 'expected_docx_map', 'full_sample_set',
    'docx_sample_set', 'overall_samples', 'overall_conditions',
    'overall_sample_conditions', 'overall_sample_files',
    'overall_sample_cycles', 'overall_cycle_minmax',
    'overall_soc_minmax', 'condition_key', 'file_summary_df',
    'conclusion_df',
    'dataset_status', 'dataset_interpretation',
    'cycle_interpretation', 'n_samples', 'n_conditions',
    'min_cycle', 'max_cycle', 'missing_vs_full', 'missing_vs_docx',
    'extra_vs_docx', 'actual_sample_set',
]
missing_objects = [name for name in required_objects if name not in globals()]
if missing_objects:
    raise RuntimeError(
        'STEP 10 requires STEPS 5, 7 and 9. Missing: '
        + ', '.join(missing_objects)
    )

qa_dir = Path(OUTPUT_DIR) / 'dataset_qa'
qa_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 1A. REBUILD STEP 8 TABLES ONLY IF THEY ARE NOT IN MEMORY
# ------------------------------------------------------------
# STEP 9 can run directly after STEP 7, so successful STEP 9
# execution does not imply that STEP 8 objects are available.
# The fallback below reproduces STEP 8 table schemas from the
# already-collected counters. No Excel workbook is opened.

def build_reconciliation_from_counters():
    reference = expected_full[[
        'Condition', 'Expected_Charge_Rate',
        'Expected_Temperature', 'Expected_Discharge_Rate',
    ]].drop_duplicates()
    if len(reference) != 24:
        raise AssertionError('Expected exactly 24 reference conditions.')

    ids = {}
    for entry in reference.itertuples(index=False):
        key = condition_key(
            entry.Expected_Charge_Rate,
            entry.Expected_Temperature,
            entry.Expected_Discharge_Rate,
        )
        if key in ids:
            raise ValueError(f'Duplicate condition key: {key}')
        ids[key] = int(entry.Condition)

    pair_rows = []
    sample_rows_local = []
    for sample_id in sorted(overall_samples):
        counter = overall_sample_conditions.get(sample_id, {})
        expected = expected_docx_map.get(sample_id)
        expected_key = (expected[1], expected[2], expected[3]) if expected else None
        expected_id = expected[0] if expected else None

        for cond, row_count in sorted(
            counter.items(),
            key=lambda item: (ids.get(item[0], 999), str(item[0])),
        ):
            if expected is None:
                status = (
                    'UNLISTED_THEORETICAL_ID'
                    if sample_id in full_sample_set
                    else 'UNKNOWN_SAMPLE_ID'
                )
            elif cond == expected_key:
                status = 'MATCH'
            else:
                status = 'CONDITION_MISMATCH'

            pair_rows.append({
                'sample_number': int(sample_id),
                'expected_condition_id': expected_id,
                'observed_condition_id': ids.get(cond),
                'observed_cutoff': cond[0],
                'observed_temperature': cond[1],
                'observed_discharge_rate': cond[2],
                'observed_rows': int(row_count),
                'n_distinct_conditions_for_sample': len(counter),
                'status': status,
                'n_source_files': len(overall_sample_files.get(sample_id, set())),
                'source_files': '; '.join(sorted(
                    Path(p).name
                    for p in overall_sample_files.get(sample_id, set())
                )),
            })

        dominant = max(
            counter.items(),
            key=lambda item: (item[1], -ids.get(item[0], 999)),
        ) if counter else None
        sample_rows_local.append({
            'sample_number': int(sample_id),
            'in_documented_map': expected is not None,
            'documented_condition_id': expected_id,
            'n_observed_conditions': len(counter),
            'dominant_observed_condition_id': (
                ids.get(dominant[0]) if dominant else None
            ),
            'dominant_condition_rows': int(dominant[1]) if dominant else 0,
            'all_condition_rows': int(sum(counter.values())),
            'n_source_files': len(overall_sample_files.get(sample_id, set())),
        })

    pair_frame = pd.DataFrame(pair_rows)
    sample_frame = pd.DataFrame(sample_rows_local)
    if pair_frame.empty:
        raise ValueError('No valid sample-condition pairs were collected in STEP 7.')

    if len(pair_frame) != sum(
        len(c) for c in overall_sample_conditions.values()
    ):
        raise AssertionError('Sample-condition pair count mismatch.')
    if int(pair_frame['observed_rows'].sum()) != sum(overall_conditions.values()):
        raise AssertionError('Condition row totals do not match STEP 7.')

    coverage_rows = []
    for condition_id in range(1, 25):
        documented_ids = set(expected_docx.loc[
            expected_docx['Condition'] == condition_id, 'sample_number'
        ].astype(int))
        observed_ids = set(pair_frame.loc[
            pair_frame['observed_condition_id'] == condition_id,
            'sample_number',
        ].astype(int))
        coverage_rows.append({
            'Condition_ID': condition_id,
            'n_documented_ids': len(documented_ids),
            'n_observed_ids_for_condition': len(observed_ids),
            'n_documented_ids_observed_in_condition': len(
                documented_ids & observed_ids
            ),
            'n_observed_ids_not_assigned_to_condition': len(
                observed_ids - documented_ids
            ),
            'missing_expected_ids_in_condition': ';'.join(map(
                str, sorted(documented_ids - observed_ids)
            )),
            'extra_observed_ids_in_condition': ';'.join(map(
                str, sorted(observed_ids - documented_ids)
            )),
        })

    return pair_frame, sample_frame, pd.DataFrame(coverage_rows), ids

step8_names = ('pairs', 'samples', 'coverage', 'condition_ids')
step8_ready = all(
    name in globals() and globals()[name] is not None
    for name in step8_names
)

if not step8_ready:
    print('[INFO] STEP 8 in-memory tables not found; reconstructing '
          'the same diagnostics from STEP 7 aggregate counters.')
    pairs, samples, coverage, condition_ids = build_reconciliation_from_counters()
    pairs.to_csv(qa_dir / 'observed_sample_condition_pairs.csv', index=False)
    samples.to_csv(qa_dir / 'observed_sample_summary.csv', index=False)
    coverage.to_csv(qa_dir / 'condition_mapping_coverage.csv', index=False)
    print('[OK] Reconstructed sample-condition pairs:', len(pairs))
    print('[OK] Reconstructed sample summary rows:', len(samples))
    print('[OK] Reconstructed condition coverage rows:', len(coverage))
else:
    print('[OK] Reusing existing STEP 8 reconciliation tables.')

# Consistency against STEP 7 observations and STEP 9 totals.
assert len(pairs) == sum(len(c) for c in overall_sample_conditions.values())
assert len(actual_sample_set) == len(overall_samples) == n_samples
assert n_conditions == len(overall_conditions)
assert set(pairs['sample_number'].astype(int)).issubset(set(overall_samples))
assert set(samples['sample_number'].astype(int)) == set(overall_samples)
assert int(pairs['observed_rows'].sum()) == sum(overall_conditions.values())
assert len(coverage) == 24

condition_rows = []
for condition_id in range(1, 25):
    full_ids = set(expected_full.loc[
        expected_full['Condition'] == condition_id, 'sample_number'
    ].astype(int))

        and observed_condition_ids[0] == expected_condition_id

        theoretical is not None
        and len(observed_condition_ids) == 1
        and observed_condition_ids[0] == theoretical[0]
    )
    cycle_bounds = overall_cycle_minmax.get(sample_id, [np.inf, -np.inf])
    soc_bounds = overall_soc_minmax.get(sample_id, [np.inf, -np.inf])

    }

        frame.to_excel(writer, index=False, sheet_name=sheet_name)

    # Apply restrained formatting without changing the underlying data.
    from openpyxl.styles import Font, PatternFill, Alignment
    from openpyxl.utils import get_column_letter

    for worksheet in writer.book.worksheets:
        worksheet.freeze_panes = 'A2'
        worksheet.auto_filter.ref = worksheet.dimensions
        for cell in worksheet[1]:
            cell.font = Font(bold=True, color='FFFFFF')
            cell.fill = PatternFill('solid', fgColor='19324D')
            cell.alignment = Alignment(vertical='center', wrap_text=True)
        worksheet.row_dimensions[1].height = 32
        for column in worksheet.columns:
            column_letter = get_column_letter(column[0].column)
            sampled_values = list(column)[:201]
            width = min(44, max(13, max(
                (len(str(cell.value)) for cell in sampled_values if cell.value is not None),
                default=11,
            ) + 2))
            worksheet.column_dimensions[column_letter].width = width

# ============================================================
# HELPERS
# ============================================================

def find_file(name):

    for root in ROOTS:

        p = os.path.join(
            root,
            name
        )

        if os.path.exists(p):
            return os.path.abspath(p)

        hits = glob.glob(
            os.path.join(
                root,
                "**",
                name
            ),
            recursive=True
        )

        if hits:

            return os.path.abspath(
                sorted(
                    hits,
                    key=lambda x: (len(x), x)
                )[0]
            )

    return None


def sample_int(x):

    try:

        s = (
            str(x)
            .strip()
            .split(".")[0]
            .lstrip("0")
        )

        return (
            int(s)
            if s
            else 0
        )

    except Exception:
        return np.nan


def norm_R(x):

    if pd.isna(x):
        return np.nan

    s = (
        str(x)
        .strip()
        .upper()
        .replace(" ", "")
        .replace("\\", "/")
    )

    aliases = {
        "C5": "C/5",
        "0.2C": "C/5",
        "0.20C": "C/5",

        "C40": "C/40",
        "0.025C": "C/40"
    }

    return aliases.get(
        s,
        s
    )


def norm_DR(x):

    if pd.isna(x):
        return np.nan

    s = (
        str(x)
        .strip()
        .upper()
        .replace(" ", "")
        .replace(",", ".")
    )

    aliases = {
        "0.7": "0.7C",
        ".7C": "0.7C",

        "1": "1C",
        "1.0": "1C",
        "1.0C": "1C",

        "2": "2C",
        "2.0": "2C",
        "2.0C": "2C"
    }

    return aliases.get(
        s,
        s
    )


def gap_safe_interp(
    soc,
    voltage,
    grid,
    max_gap=10.0
):
    """
    Interpolate only between observed neighbouring SoC values
    whose separation <= max_gap.

    No extrapolation.
    No interpolation over large missing regions.
    """

    soc = np.asarray(
        soc,
        dtype=float
    )

    voltage = np.asarray(
        voltage,
        dtype=float
    )

    valid = (
        np.isfinite(soc)
        &
        np.isfinite(voltage)
    )

    soc = soc[valid]
    voltage = voltage[valid]

    if len(soc) < 2:

        return np.full(
            len(grid),
            np.nan
        )


    # Duplicate SoC within ONE cycle:
    # robust median voltage.
    tmp = pd.DataFrame({
        "SoC": soc,
        "V": voltage
    })

    tmp = (
        tmp
        .groupby(
            "SoC",
            as_index=False,
            observed=True
        )["V"]
        .median()
        .sort_values("SoC")
    )


    x = tmp[
        "SoC"
    ].to_numpy(float)

    y = tmp[
        "V"
    ].to_numpy(float)


    out = np.full(
        len(grid),
        np.nan,
        dtype=float
    )


    if len(x) < 2:
        return out


    # Exact observed grid hits
    for j, q in enumerate(grid):

        idx_exact = np.where(
            np.isclose(
                x,
                q,
                atol=1e-9
            )
        )[0]

        if len(idx_exact):

            out[j] = y[
                idx_exact[0]
            ]

            continue


        right = np.searchsorted(
            x,
            q,
            side="right"
        )

        left = right - 1


        if (
            left < 0
            or
            right >= len(x)
        ):
            continue


        gap = (
            x[right]
            -
            x[left]
        )


        if (
            gap <= 0
            or
            gap > max_gap
        ):
            continue


        frac = (
            (q - x[left])
            /
            gap
        )

        out[j] = (
            y[left]
            +
            frac
            *
            (
                y[right]
                -
                y[left]
            )
        )


    return out


# ============================================================
# SAFE SUPPORTED-NODE CELL AGGREGATION
# ============================================================

def summarize_supported_cycle_profiles(M):
    """Return median, 25th/75th percentiles, and cycle support.

    Each column is a SoC node and each row is an eligible cycle.
    Nodes with zero finite contributing cycles retain NaN statistics.
    Only finite-voltage support is used, consistent with the original
    source-record gap masking and cell-level percentile definition.
    """
    A = np.asarray(M, dtype=float)
    if A.ndim != 2:
        raise ValueError("Cycle profiles must be a 2D array")

    n_support = np.isfinite(A).sum(axis=0)
    supported = n_support > 0
    n_nodes = A.shape[1]
    med = np.full(n_nodes, np.nan, dtype=float)
    q25 = np.full(n_nodes, np.nan, dtype=float)
    q75 = np.full(n_nodes, np.nan, dtype=float)

    if supported.any():
        values = A[:, supported]
        med[supported] = np.nanmedian(values, axis=0)
        q25[supported] = np.nanpercentile(values, 25, axis=0)
        q75[supported] = np.nanpercentile(values, 75, axis=0)

    return med, q25, q75, n_support

    for f in FILES

# ============================================================
# PASS 1
# Estimate constant-current target for every Condition_ID
#
# We use the median magnitude of NEGATIVE current under Step 5.
# Near-zero startup points have negligible effect on the median.
# ============================================================

condition_current_values = {
    cid: []
    for cid in range(1, 25)
}


print(
    "PASS 1 — estimating nominal discharge-current plateaus"
)


for k, path in enumerate(
    paths,
    start=1
):

    print(
        f"[{k}/3]",
        os.path.basename(path)
    )


    try:

        sheet = "Step5_Data"

        head = pd.read_excel(
            path,
            sheet_name=sheet,
            nrows=0,
            engine="openpyxl"
        )

    except Exception:

        sheet = 0


    d = pd.read_excel(
        path,
        sheet_name=sheet,
        usecols=REQ,
        engine="openpyxl"
    )


    for c in [
        "Temperature",
        "Step_Index",
        "CurrentA"
    ]:

        d[c] = pd.to_numeric(
            d[c],
            errors="coerce"
        )


    d["Rate"] = (
        d["Rate"]
        .map(norm_R)
    )

    d["Charge_Rate"] = (
        d["Charge_Rate"]
        .map(norm_DR)
    )


    cond = []

    for t, r, dr in zip(
        d["Temperature"],
        d["Rate"],
        d["Charge_Rate"]
    ):

        if pd.isna(t):

            cond.append(
                np.nan
            )

        else:

            cond.append(
                COND_INV.get(
                    (
                        int(round(t)),
                        r,
                        dr
                    ),
                    np.nan
                )
            )


    d["Condition_ID"] = cond


    d = d[
        (d["Step_Index"] == 5)
        &
        (d["CurrentA"] < 0)
        &
        d["Condition_ID"].notna()
    ]


    for cid, g in d.groupby(
        "Condition_ID",
        observed=True
    ):

        vals = np.abs(
            g["CurrentA"]
            .to_numpy(float)
        )

        # Exclude numerically near-zero negative transition points
        vals = vals[
            vals > 0.1
        ]

        if len(vals):

            # Store per-file median rather than all raw currents.
            condition_current_values[
                int(cid)
            ].append(
                float(
                    np.median(vals)
                )
            )


    del d
    gc.collect()


CURRENT_TARGET = {}

current_rows = []


for cid in range(1, 25):

    vals = (
        condition_current_values[
            cid
        ]
    )

    if not vals:

        CURRENT_TARGET[
            cid
        ] = np.nan

    else:

        CURRENT_TARGET[
            cid
        ] = float(
            np.median(vals)
        )


    current_rows.append({

        "Condition_ID":
            cid,

        "Temperature":
            COND_MAP[cid][0],

        "Rate":
            COND_MAP[cid][1],

        "Charge_Rate":
            COND_MAP[cid][2],

        "target_abs_current_A":
            CURRENT_TARGET[cid],

        "allowed_abs_deviation_A":
            (
                max(
                    CURRENT_ABS_TOL,
                    CURRENT_REL_TOL
                    *
                    CURRENT_TARGET[cid]
                )
                if np.isfinite(
                    CURRENT_TARGET[cid]
                )
                else np.nan
            )
    })


current_table = pd.DataFrame(
    current_rows
)

current_table.to_csv(
    os.path.join(
        OUT,
        "r2_condition_current_targets.csv"
    ),
    index=False
)


print(
    "\nEstimated current plateaus:"
)

print(
    current_table[
        [
            "Condition_ID",
            "Charge_Rate",
            "target_abs_current_A",
            "allowed_abs_deviation_A"
        ]
    ].to_string(
        index=False
    )
)


# ============================================================
# PASS 2
# Cycle-aware reconstruction
# ============================================================

print(
    "\nPASS 2 — cycle-aware reconstruction"
)


all_cell_profiles = []
all_cycle_qc = []


for k, path in enumerate(
    paths,
    start=1
):

    print(
        f"\n[{k}/3]",
        os.path.basename(path)
    )


    try:

        sheet = "Step5_Data"

        head = pd.read_excel(
            path,
            sheet_name=sheet,
            nrows=0,
            engine="openpyxl"
        )

    except Exception:

        sheet = 0


    d = pd.read_excel(
        path,
        sheet_name=sheet,
        usecols=REQ,
        engine="openpyxl"
    )


    d["__source_row__"] = np.arange(
        len(d),
        dtype=np.int32
    )


    for c in [
        "Temperature",
        "Step_Index",
        "Cycle_Index",
        "VoltageV",
        "CurrentA",
        "SoC"
    ]:

        d[c] = pd.to_numeric(
            d[c],
            errors="coerce"
        )


    d["sample_int"] = (
        d["sample_number"]
        .map(sample_int)
    )

    d["Rate"] = (
        d["Rate"]
        .map(norm_R)
    )

    d["Charge_Rate"] = (
        d["Charge_Rate"]
        .map(norm_DR)
    )


    cond = []

    for t, r, dr in zip(
        d["Temperature"],
        d["Rate"],
        d["Charge_Rate"]
    ):

        if pd.isna(t):

            cond.append(
                np.nan
            )

        else:

            cond.append(
                COND_INV.get(
                    (
                        int(round(t)),
                        r,
                        dr
                    ),
                    np.nan
                )
            )


    d["Condition_ID"] = cond


    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # Basic discharge filter
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    d = d[
        (d["Step_Index"] == 5)
        &
        (d["CurrentA"] < 0)
        &
        d["Condition_ID"].notna()
        &
        d["Cycle_Index"].notna()
        &
        d["VoltageV"].notna()
        &
        d["SoC"].notna()
        &
        d["sample_int"].notna()
    ].copy()


    d["sample_int"] = (
        d["sample_int"]
        .astype(np.int32)
    )

    d["Condition_ID"] = (
        d["Condition_ID"]
        .astype(np.int16)
    )

    d["Cycle_Index"] = (
        d["Cycle_Index"]
        .astype(np.int32)
    )


    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # Constant-current plateau filter
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    target = (
        d["Condition_ID"]
        .map(CURRENT_TARGET)
        .astype(float)
    )


    tolerance = np.maximum(
        CURRENT_ABS_TOL,
        CURRENT_REL_TOL
        *
        target
    )


    current_error = np.abs(
        np.abs(
            d["CurrentA"]
        )
        -
        target
    )


    d["on_current_plateau"] = (
        current_error
        <=
        tolerance
    )


    # Keep this diagnostic count
    n_before = len(d)

    d = d[
        d["on_current_plateau"]
    ].copy()

    n_after = len(d)


    print(
        f"  negative-current rows: {n_before:,}"
    )

    print(
        f"  plateau rows kept    : {n_after:,} "
        f"({100*n_after/n_before:.2f}%)"
    )


    d = d.sort_values(
        [
            "sample_int",
            "Condition_ID",
            "Cycle_Index",
            "__source_row__"
        ]
    )


    # ========================================================
    # Work cell-by-cell.
    # We NEVER retain all cycle-grid profiles globally.
    # ========================================================

    for (
        sid,
        cid
    ), cell_df in d.groupby(
        [
            "sample_int",
            "Condition_ID"
        ],
        sort=False,
        observed=True
    ):

        cycle_profiles = []

        condition_meta = (
            cell_df.iloc[0]
        )


        for cyc, g in cell_df.groupby(
            "Cycle_Index",
            sort=False,
            observed=True
        ):

            g = (
                g
                .sort_values(
                    "__source_row__"
                )
                .copy()
            )


            source_rows = (
                g["__source_row__"]
                .to_numpy(
                    dtype=np.int32
                )
            )

            soc = (
                g["SoC"]
                .to_numpy(float)
            )

            voltage = (
                g["VoltageV"]
                .to_numpy(float)
            )

            current = (
                g["CurrentA"]
                .to_numpy(float)
            )


            n_plateau_points = len(g)


            if n_plateau_points < MIN_POINTS_CYCLE:

                all_cycle_qc.append({

                    "sample_int":
                        int(sid),

                    "Condition_ID":
                        int(cid),

                    "Cycle_Index":
                        int(cyc),

                    "usable":
                        False,

                    "reason":
                        "too_few_plateau_points",

                    "n_plateau_points":
                        n_plateau_points
                })

                continue


            # ------------------------------------------------
            # Because non-plateau points were removed,
            # source-row gaps indicate interrupted plateau.
            #
            # Split and retain longest continuous plateau
            # segment.
            # ------------------------------------------------

            breaks = (
                np.diff(
                    source_rows
                )
                > 1
            )


            seg_id = np.concatenate(
                [
                    np.array([0]),
                    np.cumsum(
                        breaks
                    )
                ]
            )


            unique_seg, counts = np.unique(
                seg_id,
                return_counts=True
            )


            best_seg = (
                unique_seg[
                    np.argmax(
                        counts
                    )
                ]
            )


            keep = (
                seg_id
                ==
                best_seg
            )


            soc = soc[keep]
            voltage = voltage[keep]
            current = current[keep]


            if len(soc) < MIN_POINTS_CYCLE:

                all_cycle_qc.append({

                    "sample_int":
                        int(sid),

                    "Condition_ID":
                        int(cid),

                    "Cycle_Index":
                        int(cyc),

                    "usable":
                        False,

                    "reason":
                        "short_after_plateau_segmentation",

                    "n_plateau_points":
                        n_plateau_points,

                    "n_selected_points":
                        int(len(soc))
                })

                continue


            soc_span = float(
                np.nanmax(soc)
                -
                np.nanmin(soc)
            )


            if soc_span < MIN_SOC_SPAN:

                all_cycle_qc.append({

                    "sample_int":
                        int(sid),

                    "Condition_ID":
                        int(cid),

                    "Cycle_Index":
                        int(cyc),

                    "usable":
                        False,

                    "reason":
                        "insufficient_soc_span",

                    "n_plateau_points":
                        n_plateau_points,

                    "n_selected_points":
                        int(len(soc)),

                    "soc_span":
                        soc_span
                })

                continue


            # ------------------------------------------------
            # Cycle diagnostics BEFORE interpolation
            # ------------------------------------------------

            dsoc = np.diff(soc)
            dv = np.diff(voltage)


            valid_down = (
                dsoc < 0
            )


            reverse50 = int(
                np.sum(
                    valid_down
                    &
                    (dv >= 0.050)
                )
            )


            very_large_gap = int(
                np.sum(
                    np.abs(dsoc)
                    >
                    MAX_INTERP_GAP_SOC
                )
            )


            # ------------------------------------------------
            # GAP-SAFE interpolation
            # ------------------------------------------------

            profile = gap_safe_interp(
                soc=soc,
                voltage=voltage,
                grid=SOC_GRID,
                max_gap=MAX_INTERP_GAP_SOC
            )


            n_grid = int(
                np.isfinite(
                    profile
                ).sum()
            )


            if n_grid < 5:

                all_cycle_qc.append({

                    "sample_int":
                        int(sid),

                    "Condition_ID":
                        int(cid),

                    "Cycle_Index":
                        int(cyc),

                    "usable":
                        False,

                    "reason":
                        "insufficient_interpolated_support",

                    "n_selected_points":
                        int(len(soc)),

                    "n_grid_points":
                        n_grid
                })

                continue


            cycle_profiles.append(
                profile
            )


            all_cycle_qc.append({

                "sample_int":
                    int(sid),

                "Condition_ID":
                    int(cid),

                "Cycle_Index":
                    int(cyc),

                "usable":
                    True,

                "reason":
                    "ok",

                "n_plateau_points":
                    n_plateau_points,

                "n_selected_points":
                    int(len(soc)),

                "soc_min":
                    float(
                        np.min(soc)
                    ),

                "soc_max":
                    float(
                        np.max(soc)
                    ),

                "soc_span":
                    soc_span,

                "n_reverse_ge50mV":
                    reverse50,

                "n_gaps_gt10pct":
                    very_large_gap,

                "n_grid_points":
                    n_grid
            })


        # ====================================================
        # Robust aggregation:
        # cycles -> ONE CELL profile
        # ====================================================

        if not cycle_profiles:
            continue


        M = np.asarray(
            cycle_profiles,
            dtype=float
        )


        # Compute statistics only at supported SoC nodes.
        # Unsupported nodes remain NaN, as in the original method.
        # This avoids repetitive all-NaN slice warnings without
        # changing which nodes contribute to the cell profile.
        (
            cell_median,
            cell_q25,
            cell_q75,
            n_cycles_soc,
        ) = summarize_supported_cycle_profiles(M)


        for j, soc_q in enumerate(
            SOC_GRID
        ):

            all_cell_profiles.append({

                "sample_int":
                    int(sid),

                "Condition_ID":
                    int(cid),

                "Temperature":
                    int(
                        condition_meta[
                            "Temperature"
                        ]
                    ),

                "Rate":
                    str(
                        condition_meta[
                            "Rate"
                        ]
                    ),

                "Charge_Rate":
                    str(
                        condition_meta[
                            "Charge_Rate"
                        ]
                    ),

                "SoC":
                    float(soc_q),

                "V_cell_median":
                    (
                        float(
                            cell_median[j]
                        )
                        if np.isfinite(
                            cell_median[j]
                        )
                        else np.nan
                    ),

                "V_cell_q25":
                    (
                        float(
                            cell_q25[j]
                        )
                        if np.isfinite(
                            cell_q25[j]
                        )
                        else np.nan
                    ),

                "V_cell_q75":
                    (
                        float(
                            cell_q75[j]
                        )
                        if np.isfinite(
                            cell_q75[j]
                        )
                        else np.nan
                    ),

                "n_cycles_at_soc":
                    int(
                        n_cycles_soc[j]
                    )
            })


        del M
        del cycle_profiles


    del d
    gc.collect()


# ============================================================
# SAVE FINAL TABLES
# ============================================================

cell_profiles = pd.DataFrame(
    all_cell_profiles
)

cycle_qc = pd.DataFrame(
    all_cycle_qc
)


cell_profiles.to_csv(
    os.path.join(
        OUT,
        "r2_cell_profiles.csv"
    ),
    index=False
)


cycle_qc.to_csv(
    os.path.join(
        OUT,
        "r2_cycle_qc.csv"
    ),
    index=False
)


# ============================================================
# CONDITION/CYCLE COVERAGE
# ============================================================

usable_qc = cycle_qc[
    cycle_qc["usable"] == True
].copy()


coverage = (

    usable_qc

    .groupby(
        [
            "Condition_ID",
            "sample_int"
        ],
        as_index=False,
        observed=True
    )

    .agg(

        n_usable_cycles=(
            "Cycle_Index",
            "nunique"
        ),

        cycle_min=(
            "Cycle_Index",
            "min"
        ),

        cycle_median=(
            "Cycle_Index",
            "median"
        ),

        cycle_max=(
            "Cycle_Index",
            "max"
        ),

        cycles_with_reverse50=(
            "n_reverse_ge50mV",
            lambda x:
                int(
                    np.sum(
                        np.asarray(x)
                        > 0
                    )
                )
        ),

        total_large_gaps=(
            "n_gaps_gt10pct",
            "sum"
        )
    )

)


coverage.to_csv(
    os.path.join(
        OUT,
        "r2_condition_cycle_coverage.csv"
    ),
    index=False
)


# ============================================================

# ============================================================

print(
    "\n"
    + "=" * 70
)

print(
    "R2 FILE-LOCAL RECONSTRUCTION COMPLETE — R2.2 REQUIRED"
)

print(
    "=" * 70
)


print(
    "\nCell profiles:",
    f"{len(cell_profiles):,}"
)

    cell_profiles[
        "sample_int"
    ].nunique()
)

print(

    cell_profiles[
        "Condition_ID"
    ].nunique()
)

    "\nCycle QC:"
)

print(
    cycle_qc[
        "usable"
    ].value_counts(
        dropna=False
    )
)

    "\nReasons:"
)

print(
    cycle_qc[
        "reason"
    ].value_counts()
)


print(
    "\nCycles with >=1 reverse >=50 mV event:"
)

print(
    int(
        (
            usable_qc[
                "n_reverse_ge50mV"
            ]
            > 0
        ).sum()
    ),
    "/",
    len(
        usable_qc
    )
)


print(
    "\nCycles containing >10% SoC gaps:"
)

print(
    int(
        (
            usable_qc[
                "n_gaps_gt10pct"
            ]
            > 0
        ).sum()
    ),
    "/",
    len(
        usable_qc
    )
)

]:

    print(
        " -",
        os.path.join(

            f
        )
    )

"""R2.1 — Audit nominal sample-to-condition assignments in the R2 outputs.

This descriptive quality-assurance stage reads only the R2 CSV artifacts.
It does not modify source data, select reconstruction cycles, or change
any manuscript analysis. The five original CSV outputs are preserved.

Usage (Colab): run this cell/script after R2 output files have been saved.
Usage (CLI): SOC_R2_DIR=/path/to/revision_R2_final_profiles python script.py
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import os
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd


# Match the CALCE sample assignment recorded in the frozen manuscript.
# Conditions 1–22 have eight documented IDs each; conditions 23–24
# have three each. Samples 180–184 and 188–192 have no documented map.
NOMINAL_SAMPLE_RANGES = tuple(
    [(8 * (cid - 1) + 1, 8 * cid, cid) for cid in range(1, 23)]
    + [(177, 179, 23), (185, 187, 24)]
)

CELL_COLUMNS = (
    "sample_int", "Condition_ID", "Temperature", "Rate", "Charge_Rate"
)
QC_COLUMNS = ("sample_int", "Condition_ID", "Cycle_Index", "usable")
OUTPUT_NAMES = (
    "conditions_per_sample.csv",
    "observed_sample_condition_pairs.csv",
    "nominal_condition_mismatches.csv",
    "cells_per_condition.csv",
    "sample_condition_cycle_support.csv",
)


def nominal_condition(sample_id: int) -> float | int:
    """Return the documented condition, or NaN for an unmapped sample ID."""
    sid = int(sample_id)
    for lower, upper, cid in NOMINAL_SAMPLE_RANGES:
        if lower <= sid <= upper:
            return cid
    return np.nan


def _join_sorted_ids(values: pd.Series) -> str:
    return ",".join(str(int(x)) for x in sorted(pd.unique(values)))


def _require_columns(path: Path, columns: tuple[str, ...]) -> None:
    if not path.is_file():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"Missing R2 input artifact: {path}")
    header = pd.read_csv(path, nrows=0)
    missing = sorted(set(columns) - set(header.columns))
    if missing:
