        raise ValueError(f"Missing required columns in {path.name}: {missing}")


def _normalize_boolean(values: pd.Series) -> pd.Series:
    """Read the documented R2 usable flag safely across CSV dtype inference."""
    normalized = values.astype("string").str.strip().str.lower()
    accepted = {"true": True, "false": False, "1": True, "0": False}
    invalid = ~normalized.isin(accepted)
    if bool(invalid.any()):
        bad = values[invalid].head(5).tolist()
        raise ValueError(f"Unknown values in R2 cycle QC 'usable' column: {bad}")
    return normalized.map(accepted).astype(bool)


def run_audit(r2_directory: str | Path) -> dict[str, pd.DataFrame]:
    """Recreate the original five R2.1 audit CSVs without altering inputs."""
    r2_dir = Path(r2_directory).expanduser()
    cell_path = r2_dir / "r2_cell_profiles.csv"
    qc_path = r2_dir / "r2_cycle_qc.csv"
    out = r2_dir / "R2_1_design_audit"

    _require_columns(cell_path, CELL_COLUMNS)
    _require_columns(qc_path, QC_COLUMNS)

    cell = pd.read_csv(cell_path, usecols=list(CELL_COLUMNS))
    qc = pd.read_csv(qc_path, usecols=list(QC_COLUMNS))
    qc["usable"] = _normalize_boolean(qc["usable"])

    for label, frame in (("cell profiles", cell), ("cycle QC", qc)):
        for key in ("sample_int", "Condition_ID"):
            key_numeric = pd.to_numeric(frame[key], errors="coerce")
            if key_numeric.isna().any() or not (key_numeric % 1 == 0).all():
                raise ValueError(f"Invalid {key} in {label}")
            frame[key] = key_numeric.astype("int64")

    pairs = (
        cell[list(CELL_COLUMNS)]
        .drop_duplicates()
        .sort_values(["sample_int", "Condition_ID"])
        .reset_index(drop=True)
    )
    # A sample-condition pair must have a unique factor assignment in R2.
    uniqueness = pairs.groupby(["sample_int", "Condition_ID"], observed=True).size()
    if (uniqueness > 1).any():
        raise ValueError("Conflicting factor metadata for a sample-condition pair")

    sample_condition_count = (
        pairs.groupby("sample_int", as_index=False, observed=True)
        .agg(
            n_conditions=("Condition_ID", "nunique"),
            observed_conditions=("Condition_ID", _join_sorted_ids),
        )
    )
    multi = sample_condition_count.loc[
        sample_condition_count["n_conditions"] > 1
    ].copy()

    pairs["Nominal_Condition_ID"] = pairs["sample_int"].map(nominal_condition)
    pairs["matches_nominal"] = (
        pairs["Condition_ID"] == pairs["Nominal_Condition_ID"]
    )
    known_nominal = pairs.loc[pairs["Nominal_Condition_ID"].notna()]
    unknown_nominal = pairs.loc[pairs["Nominal_Condition_ID"].isna()]
    matches = known_nominal.loc[known_nominal["matches_nominal"]]
    mismatches = known_nominal.loc[~known_nominal["matches_nominal"]].copy()

    condition_cells = (
        pairs.groupby(
            ["Condition_ID", "Temperature", "Rate", "Charge_Rate"],
            as_index=False,
            observed=True,
        )
        .agg(
            n_observed_cells=("sample_int", "nunique"),
            observed_samples=("sample_int", _join_sorted_ids),
        )
        .sort_values("Condition_ID")
    )

    usable = qc.loc[qc["usable"]].copy()
    pair_cycle_support = (
        usable.groupby(["sample_int", "Condition_ID"], as_index=False, observed=True)
        .agg(
            n_usable_cycles=("Cycle_Index", "nunique"),
            cycle_min=("Cycle_Index", "min"),
            cycle_median=("Cycle_Index", "median"),
            cycle_max=("Cycle_Index", "max"),
        )
    )
    # Every accepted physical cycle must belong to a reconstructed cell profile.
    paired_keys = pairs[["sample_int", "Condition_ID"]]
    missing_pairs = pair_cycle_support.merge(
        paired_keys, on=["sample_int", "Condition_ID"], how="left", indicator=True
    )
    if (missing_pairs["_merge"] == "left_only").any():
        raise ValueError("Accepted QC cycles lack a corresponding R2 cell profile")

    pair_audit = pairs.merge(
        pair_cycle_support,
        on=["sample_int", "Condition_ID"],
        how="left",
    )

    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    tables = {
        OUTPUT_NAMES[0]: sample_condition_count,
        OUTPUT_NAMES[1]: pairs,
        OUTPUT_NAMES[2]: mismatches,
        OUTPUT_NAMES[3]: condition_cells,
        OUTPUT_NAMES[4]: pair_audit,
    }
    for filename, frame in tables.items():
        frame.to_csv(out / filename, index=False)

    print("=" * 70)
    print("R2.1 DESIGN CONSISTENCY AUDIT COMPLETE")
    print("=" * 70)
    print(f"Observed sample-condition pairs: {len(pairs):,}")
    print(f"Unique physical sample IDs: {pairs['sample_int'].nunique():,}")
    print(f"Observed conditions: {pairs['Condition_ID'].nunique():,}")
    print(f"Samples assigned to multiple conditions: {len(multi):,}")
    print(f"Nominally concordant pairs: {len(matches):,}")
    print(f"Mismatched documented pairs: {len(mismatches):,}")
    print(f"Unmapped sample-condition pairs: {len(unknown_nominal):,}")
    fraction = float(known_nominal["matches_nominal"].mean()) if len(known_nominal) else np.nan
    print(f"Nominal match fraction among mapped pairs: {fraction:.6f}")
    print(f"R2 usable cycle records audited: {len(usable):,}")
    print("\nSamples assigned to multiple conditions:")
    print(multi.head(40).to_string(index=False) if not multi.empty else "None")
    print("\nFirst 50 nominal assignment mismatches:")
    columns = (
        "sample_int", "Nominal_Condition_ID", "Condition_ID",
        "Temperature", "Rate", "Charge_Rate",
    )
    print(mismatches[list(columns)].head(50).to_string(index=False) if not mismatches.empty else "None")
    print("\nObserved cells per condition:")
    print(condition_cells.to_string(index=False))
    print(f"\nOutput directory: {out}")
    print("Source R2 artifacts were not modified.")
    return tables


if __name__ == "__main__":
    run_audit(
        os.environ.get(
            "SOC_R2_DIR",
            "/content/revision_R2_final_profiles"
            if Path("/content").is_dir() else "revision_R2_final_profiles",
        )
    )

# ============================================================
# REVISION R2.2 — CLEAN REPRODUCIBILITY VERSION
# GLOBAL CYCLE STORE, CROSS-FILE DEDUPLICATION,
# OBSERVED-METADATA AND NOMINAL-CONCORDANT BRANCHES
#
# Execute after the validated R2 reconstruction in the same Colab
# session. This script preserves the original selection, fitting,
# float32 storage, and output conventions.
#
# Manuscript is frozen: the QA checks verify outcomes but never
# change scientific selection rules to force a particular count.
#
# Main goals:
# 1) One row = one physical cell × condition × cycle
# 2) Remove accidental cross-file duplicate cycles
# 3) Build exact global cell profiles across ALL files
# 4) Produce:
#       A) observed-metadata branch
#       B) nominal-concordant branch
#
# Memory-safe wide format:
# ~89k rows × 101 voltage columns
# ============================================================

import os
import gc
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

OUT22 = os.environ.get(
    "SOC_R22_OUTPUT_DIR",
    "/content/revision_R2_2_global" if os.path.isdir("/content")
    else "outputs/revision_R2_2_global",
)
os.makedirs(OUT22, exist_ok=True)

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Prerequisite check
# ------------------------------------------------------------

required_globals = [
    "paths",
    "REQ",
    "CURRENT_TARGET",
    "COND_INV",
    "sample_int",
    "norm_R",
    "norm_DR",
    "gap_safe_interp"
]

missing = [
    x for x in required_globals
    if x not in globals()
]

if missing:
    raise RuntimeError(
        "R2 must be run first. Missing: "
        + ", ".join(missing)
    )


SOC_GRID22 = np.arange(
    0.0,
    101.0,
    1.0
)

V_COLS = [
    f"V_{i:03d}"
    for i in range(101)
]

CURRENT_REL_TOL22 = 0.03
CURRENT_ABS_TOL22 = 0.05
MAX_GAP22 = 10.0
MIN_POINTS22 = 5
MIN_SOC_SPAN22 = 5.0


# ============================================================
# NOMINAL MAP
# ============================================================

def nominal_condition(sample_id):

    s = int(sample_id)

    ranges = [
        (1,   8,   1),
        (9,   16,  2),
        (17,  24,  3),
        (25,  32,  4),
        (33,  40,  5),
        (41,  48,  6),
        (49,  56,  7),
        (57,  64,  8),
        (65,  72,  9),
        (73,  80, 10),
        (81,  88, 11),
        (89,  96, 12),
        (97, 104, 13),
        (105,112, 14),
        (113,120, 15),
        (121,128, 16),
        (129,136, 17),
        (137,144, 18),
        (145,152, 19),
        (153,160, 20),
        (161,168, 21),
        (169,176, 22),
        (177,179, 23),
        (185,187, 24)
    ]

    for lo, hi, cid in ranges:

        if lo <= s <= hi:
            return cid

    return np.nan




# ============================================================
# SUPPORTED-NODE AGGREGATION (WARNING-FREE)
# ============================================================

def summarize_supported_profile_nodes(profiles):
    """Compute original nodewise median, Q25, Q75, and support.

    A SoC node with no finite cycle voltages retains NaN in every
    summary. This avoids all-NaN percentile warnings without adding
    support, extrapolating, or altering the underlying aggregation.
    """
    array = np.asarray(profiles, dtype=float)
    if array.ndim != 2:
        raise ValueError("Expected a two-dimensional cycle-profile array")

    n_support = np.isfinite(array).sum(axis=0)
    supported = n_support > 0
    n_nodes = array.shape[1]
    median = np.full(n_nodes, np.nan, dtype=float)
    q25 = np.full(n_nodes, np.nan, dtype=float)
    q75 = np.full(n_nodes, np.nan, dtype=float)
    if supported.any():
        values = array[:, supported]
        median[supported] = np.nanmedian(values, axis=0)
        q25[supported] = np.nanpercentile(values, 25, axis=0)
        q75[supported] = np.nanpercentile(values, 75, axis=0)

    return median, q25, q75, n_support

# ============================================================
# PROCESS EACH WORKBOOK
# ============================================================

part_files = []

for file_no, path in enumerate(
    paths,
    start=1
):

    source_name = os.path.basename(
        path
    )

    print(
        f"\n[{file_no}/3] Processing:",
        source_name
    )

    try:

        sheet = "Step5_Data"

        pd.read_excel(
            path,
            sheet_name=sheet,
            nrows=0,
            engine="openpyxl"
        )

    except Exception:

        sheet = 0


    d = pd.read_excel(
        path,
        sheet_name=sheet,
        usecols=REQ,
        engine="openpyxl"
    )

    d["__source_row__"] = np.arange(
        len(d),
        dtype=np.int32
    )


    for c in [
        "Temperature",
        "Step_Index",
        "Cycle_Index",
        "VoltageV",
        "CurrentA",
        "SoC"
    ]:

        d[c] = pd.to_numeric(
            d[c],
            errors="coerce"
        )


    d["sample_int"] = (
        d["sample_number"]
        .map(sample_int)
    )

    d["Rate"] = (
        d["Rate"]
        .map(norm_R)
    )

    d["Charge_Rate"] = (
        d["Charge_Rate"]
        .map(norm_DR)
    )


    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # Observed metadata -> Condition_ID
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    cond = []

    for t, r, dr in zip(
        d["Temperature"],
        d["Rate"],
        d["Charge_Rate"]
    ):

        if pd.isna(t):

            cond.append(
                np.nan
            )

        else:

            cond.append(
                COND_INV.get(
                    (
                        int(round(t)),
                        r,
                        dr
                    ),
                    np.nan
                )
            )

    d["Condition_ID"] = cond


    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # Discharge rows
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    d = d[
        (d["Step_Index"] == 5)
        &
        (d["CurrentA"] < 0)
        &
        d["Condition_ID"].notna()
        &
        d["Cycle_Index"].notna()
        &
        d["VoltageV"].notna()
        &
        d["SoC"].notna()
        &
        d["sample_int"].notna()
    ].copy()


    d["sample_int"] = (
        d["sample_int"]
        .astype(np.int32)
    )

    d["Condition_ID"] = (
        d["Condition_ID"]
        .astype(np.int16)
    )

    d["Cycle_Index"] = (
        d["Cycle_Index"]
        .astype(np.int32)
    )


    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # Nominal current plateau
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    target = (
        d["Condition_ID"]
        .map(CURRENT_TARGET)
        .astype(float)
    )

    tol = np.maximum(
        CURRENT_ABS_TOL22,
        CURRENT_REL_TOL22
        *
        target
    )

    current_error = np.abs(
        np.abs(
            d["CurrentA"]
        )
        -
        target
    )

    d = d[
        current_error <= tol
    ].copy()


    d = d.sort_values(
        [
            "sample_int",
            "Condition_ID",
            "Cycle_Index",
            "__source_row__"
        ]
    )


    meta_rows = []
    profile_rows = []


    # ========================================================
    # ONE GROUP = ONE OBSERVED PHYSICAL CYCLE
    # ========================================================

    for (
        sid,
        cid,
        cyc
    ), g in d.groupby(
        [
            "sample_int",
            "Condition_ID",
            "Cycle_Index"
        ],
        sort=False,
        observed=True
    ):

        g = g.sort_values(
            "__source_row__"
        )

        rows = (
            g["__source_row__"]
            .to_numpy(
                dtype=np.int32
            )
        )

        soc = (
            g["SoC"]
            .to_numpy(float)
        )

        voltage = (
            g["VoltageV"]
            .to_numpy(float)
        )


        if len(g) < MIN_POINTS22:
            continue


        # ----------------------------------------------------
        # Longest contiguous plateau segment
        # ----------------------------------------------------

        breaks = (
            np.diff(rows) > 1
        )

        seg = np.concatenate(
            [
                [0],
                np.cumsum(
                    breaks
                )
            ]
        )

        ids, counts = np.unique(
            seg,
            return_counts=True
        )

        best = ids[
            np.argmax(counts)
        ]

        keep = (
            seg == best
        )

        soc = soc[keep]
        voltage = voltage[keep]


        if len(soc) < MIN_POINTS22:
            continue


        soc_span = (
            np.nanmax(soc)
            -
            np.nanmin(soc)
        )

        if soc_span < MIN_SOC_SPAN22:
            continue


        profile = gap_safe_interp(
            soc=soc,
            voltage=voltage,
            grid=SOC_GRID22,
            max_gap=MAX_GAP22
        )


        n_grid = int(
            np.isfinite(
                profile
            ).sum()
        )

        if n_grid < 5:
            continue


        row0 = g.iloc[0]

        meta_rows.append({
            "__source_file__":
                source_name,

            "sample_int":
                int(sid),

            "Condition_ID":
                int(cid),

            "Cycle_Index":
                int(cyc),

            "Temperature":
                int(
                    row0[
                        "Temperature"
                    ]
                ),

            "Rate":
                str(
                    row0[
                        "Rate"
                    ]
                ),

            "Charge_Rate":
                str(
                    row0[
                        "Charge_Rate"
                    ]
                ),

            "n_grid_points":
                n_grid
        })

        profile_rows.append(
            profile.astype(
                np.float32
            )
        )


    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # Build wide DataFrame for this workbook
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    meta_df = pd.DataFrame(
        meta_rows
    )

    if not profile_rows:
        raise ValueError(
            f"No usable discharge cycle profiles in {source_name}. "
            "Inspect source metadata and the current-plateau filter."
        )

    V = np.vstack(
        profile_rows
    )

    V_df = pd.DataFrame(
        V,
        columns=V_COLS
    )

    part = pd.concat(
        [
            meta_df.reset_index(
                drop=True
            ),
            V_df
        ],
        axis=1
    )


    part_path = os.path.join(
        OUT22,
        f"cycle_profiles_part_{file_no}.pkl"
    )

    part.to_pickle(
        part_path
    )

    part_files.append(
        part_path
    )


    print(
        "  usable cycle profiles:",
        f"{len(part):,}"
    )


    del d
    del meta_df
    del V
    del V_df
    del part
    del meta_rows
    del profile_rows

    gc.collect()


# ============================================================
# COMBINE ALL THREE WORKBOOKS
# ============================================================

print(
    "\nCombining workbook-level cycle tables..."
)

cycle = pd.concat(
    [
        pd.read_pickle(p)
        for p in part_files
    ],
    ignore_index=True
)

print(
    "Total cycle rows before cross-file dedup:",
    f"{len(cycle):,}"
)


# ============================================================
# CROSS-FILE DUPLICATE CYCLE AUDIT
# ============================================================

KEY = [
    "sample_int",
    "Condition_ID",
    "Cycle_Index"
]

dup_count = (
    cycle
    .groupby(
        KEY,
        observed=True
    )
    .size()
    .rename(
        "n_source_occurrences"
    )
    .reset_index()
)

dup_keys = dup_count[
    dup_count[
        "n_source_occurrences"
    ] > 1
].copy()

print(
    "Duplicate physical cycle keys:",
    len(dup_keys)
)


duplicate_audit = []


for _, keyrow in dup_keys.iterrows():

    mask = (
        (cycle["sample_int"] == keyrow["sample_int"])
        &
        (cycle["Condition_ID"] == keyrow["Condition_ID"])
        &
        (cycle["Cycle_Index"] == keyrow["Cycle_Index"])
    )

    g = cycle[
        mask
    ]

    A = g[
        V_COLS
    ].to_numpy(
        dtype=float
    )

    reference = A[0]

    maes = []
    maxes = []

    for j in range(
        1,
        len(A)
    ):

        common = (
            np.isfinite(reference)
            &
            np.isfinite(A[j])
        )

        if common.sum() == 0:

            maes.append(
                np.nan
            )

            maxes.append(
                np.nan
            )

        else:

            diff = np.abs(
                reference[common]
                -
                A[j][common]
            )

            maes.append(
                float(
                    np.mean(diff)
                )
            )

            maxes.append(
                float(
                    np.max(diff)
                )
            )


    duplicate_audit.append({

        "sample_int":
            int(
                keyrow["sample_int"]
            ),

        "Condition_ID":
            int(
                keyrow["Condition_ID"]
            ),

        "Cycle_Index":
            int(
                keyrow["Cycle_Index"]
            ),

        "n_occurrences":
            int(len(g)),

        "source_files":
            "|".join(
                g[
                    "__source_file__"
                ]
                .astype(str)
                .tolist()
            ),

        "max_pairwise_MAE_V":
            (
                np.nanmax(maes)
                if len(maes)
                else 0.0
            ),

        "max_pairwise_abs_V":
            (
                np.nanmax(maxes)
                if len(maxes)
                else 0.0
            )
    })


duplicate_audit = pd.DataFrame(
    duplicate_audit
)


duplicate_audit.to_csv(
    os.path.join(
        OUT22,
        "duplicate_cycle_audit.csv"
    ),
    index=False
)


if len(
    duplicate_audit
):

    print(
        "\n=== DUPLICATE CYCLE AUDIT ==="
    )

    print(
        duplicate_audit[
            [
                "n_occurrences",
                "max_pairwise_MAE_V",
                "max_pairwise_abs_V"
            ]
        ]
        .describe()
        .to_string()
    )


# ============================================================
# DEDUP RULE
#
# Only auto-collapse cycles that are practically identical:
# MAE <=1 mV AND max difference <=5 mV.
#
# Materially different duplicate cycles are NOT silently merged.
# ============================================================

if len(
    duplicate_audit
):

    material = duplicate_audit[
        (
            duplicate_audit[
                "max_pairwise_MAE_V"
            ] > 0.001
        )
        |
        (
            duplicate_audit[
                "max_pairwise_abs_V"
            ] > 0.005
        )
    ].copy()

else:

    material = pd.DataFrame()


print(
    "\nMaterially different duplicate cycle keys:",
    len(material)
)


if len(material) > 0:

    material.to_csv(
        os.path.join(
            OUT22,
            "MATERIAL_duplicate_cycles.csv"
        ),
        index=False
    )

    print(
        "\nSTOP: Some identical cell-condition-cycle keys "
        "contain materially different profiles."
    )

    print(
        "Inspect MATERIAL_duplicate_cycles.csv before proceeding."
    )

    GLOBAL_OK = False

else:

    # Drop practically identical duplicates
    cycle = (
        cycle
        .sort_values(
            [
                "sample_int",
                "Condition_ID",
                "Cycle_Index",
                "n_grid_points"
            ],
            ascending=[
                True,
                True,
                True,
                False
            ]
        )
        .drop_duplicates(
            subset=KEY,
            keep="first"
        )
        .reset_index(
            drop=True
        )
    )

    GLOBAL_OK = True


# ============================================================
# CONTINUE ONLY IF CROSS-FILE CYCLE IDENTITY IS CLEAN
# ============================================================

if GLOBAL_OK:

    print(
        "\nCycle rows after dedup:",
        f"{len(cycle):,}"
    )


    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # Nominal-concordance markers
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    cycle[
        "Nominal_Condition_ID"
    ] = (
        cycle[
            "sample_int"
        ]
        .map(
            nominal_condition
        )
    )


    cycle[
        "matches_nominal"
    ] = (
        cycle[
            "Nominal_Condition_ID"
        ].notna()
        &
        (
            cycle[
                "Condition_ID"
            ]
            ==
            cycle[
                "Nominal_Condition_ID"
            ]
        )
    )


    # Save global cycle store
    cycle.to_pickle(
        os.path.join(
            OUT22,
            "global_cycle_profiles.pkl"
        )
    )


    # ========================================================
    # CELL-PROFILE BUILDER
    # ========================================================

    def build_global_cell_profiles(
        cyc,
        branch_name
    ):

        records = []

        for (
            sid,
            cid
        ), g in cyc.groupby(
            [
                "sample_int",
                "Condition_ID"
            ],
            observed=True,
            sort=True
        ):

            A = g[
                V_COLS
            ].to_numpy(
                dtype=float
            )


            med, q25, q75, n_support = (
                summarize_supported_profile_nodes(A)
            )


            meta = g.iloc[0]

            for j, soc in enumerate(
                SOC_GRID22
            ):

                records.append({

                    "branch":
                        branch_name,

                    "sample_int":
                        int(sid),

                    "Condition_ID":
                        int(cid),

                    "Temperature":
                        int(
                            meta[
                                "Temperature"
                            ]
                        ),

                    "Rate":
                        str(
                            meta[
                                "Rate"
                            ]
                        ),

                    "Charge_Rate":
                        str(
                            meta[
                                "Charge_Rate"
                            ]
                        ),

                    "SoC":
                        float(soc),

                    "V_cell_median":
                        (
                            float(med[j])
                            if np.isfinite(
                                med[j]
                            )
                            else np.nan
                        ),

                    "V_cell_q25":
                        (
                            float(q25[j])
                            if np.isfinite(
                                q25[j]
                            )
                            else np.nan
                        ),

                    "V_cell_q75":
                        (
                            float(q75[j])
                            if np.isfinite(
                                q75[j]
                            )
                            else np.nan
                        ),

                    "n_cycles_at_soc":
                        int(
                            n_support[j]
                        )
                })


        return pd.DataFrame(
            records
        )


    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # A) Observed metadata branch
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    observed_cycles = (
        cycle.copy()
    )

    observed_profiles = (
        build_global_cell_profiles(
            observed_cycles,
            "observed_metadata"
        )
    )


    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # B) Nominal-concordant branch
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    nominal_cycles = cycle[
        cycle[
            "matches_nominal"
        ]
    ].copy()

    nominal_profiles = (
        build_global_cell_profiles(
            nominal_cycles,
            "nominal_concordant"
        )
    )


    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # Save
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    observed_profiles.to_csv(
        os.path.join(
            OUT22,
            "cell_profiles_observed_metadata.csv"
        ),
        index=False
    )

    nominal_profiles.to_csv(
        os.path.join(
            OUT22,
            "cell_profiles_nominal_concordant.csv"
        ),
        index=False
    )


    # ========================================================
    # BRANCH SUMMARY
    # ========================================================

    summary = pd.DataFrame([

        {
            "branch":
                "observed_metadata",

            "n_cycles":
                len(
                    observed_cycles
                ),

            "n_cells":
                observed_cycles[
                    "sample_int"
                ].nunique(),

            "n_sample_condition_pairs":
                observed_cycles[
                    [
                        "sample_int",
                        "Condition_ID"
                    ]
                ]
                .drop_duplicates()
                .shape[0],

            "n_conditions":
                observed_cycles[
                    "Condition_ID"
                ].nunique()
        },

        {
            "branch":
                "nominal_concordant",

            "n_cycles":
                len(
                    nominal_cycles
                ),

            "n_cells":
                nominal_cycles[
                    "sample_int"
                ].nunique(),

            "n_sample_condition_pairs":
                nominal_cycles[
                    [
                        "sample_int",
                        "Condition_ID"
                    ]
                ]
                .drop_duplicates()
                .shape[0],

            "n_conditions":
                nominal_cycles[
                    "Condition_ID"
                ].nunique()
        }
    ])


    summary.to_csv(
        os.path.join(
            OUT22,
            "branch_summary.csv"
        ),
        index=False
    )


    # Per-condition cells
    cond_summary = (

        nominal_cycles[
            [
                "sample_int",
                "Condition_ID"
            ]
        ]

        .drop_duplicates()

        .groupby(
            "Condition_ID",
            as_index=False
        )

        .agg(
            n_nominal_cells=(
                "sample_int",
                "nunique"
            )
        )
    )


    cond_summary.to_csv(
        os.path.join(
            OUT22,
            "nominal_cells_per_condition.csv"
        ),
        index=False
    )


    print(
        "\n"
        + "=" * 70
    )

    print(
        "R2.2 COMPLETE"
    )

    print(
        "=" * 70
    )

    print(
        "\n=== BRANCH SUMMARY ==="
    )

    print(
        summary.to_string(
            index=False
        )
    )

    print(
        "\n=== NOMINAL CELLS PER CONDITION ==="
    )

    print(
        cond_summary.to_string(
            index=False
        )
    )

    print(
        "\nObserved profile rows:",
        f"{len(observed_profiles):,}"
    )

    print(
        "Nominal-concordant profile rows:",
        f"{len(nominal_profiles):,}"
    )

    print(
        "\nOutput directory:",
        OUT22
    )


# ============================================================
# FINAL SCIENTIFIC REPRODUCIBILITY CHECKS
# ============================================================
# These are post hoc checks on the already reconstructed outputs.
# They do not determine which records are retained.

if GLOBAL_OK:
    expected_branch_counts = {
        "observed_metadata": (84195, 181, 203, 24),
        "nominal_concordant": (83045, 180, 180, 24),
    }
    observed_branch_counts = {
        str(record.branch): (
            int(record.n_cycles), int(record.n_cells),
            int(record.n_sample_condition_pairs), int(record.n_conditions)
        )
        for record in summary.itertuples(index=False)
    }
    expected_cells_per_condition = {
        condition_id: (7 if condition_id in (3, 4)
                       else 3 if condition_id in (23, 24)
                       else 8)
        for condition_id in range(1, 25)
    }
    observed_cells_per_condition = dict(zip(
        cond_summary["Condition_ID"].astype(int),
        cond_summary["n_nominal_cells"].astype(int),
    ))

    print("\n" + "=" * 70)
    print("R2.2 — MANUSCRIPT REPRODUCIBILITY AUDIT")
    print("=" * 70)
    print(f"Unique duplicate cycle keys: {len(dup_keys):,}")
    print(f"Nominally excluded cycles: {len(observed_cycles)-len(nominal_cycles):,}")
    print(f"Output profile rows: observed={len(observed_profiles):,}, "
          f"nominal={len(nominal_profiles):,}")

    valid = (
        observed_branch_counts == expected_branch_counts
        and observed_cells_per_condition == expected_cells_per_condition
        and len(dup_keys) == 0
        and len(observed_profiles) == 20503
        and len(nominal_profiles) == 18180
    )
    print("Manuscript-reference invariants:", "PASS" if valid else "MISMATCH")
    if not valid:
        print("Observed branches:", observed_branch_counts)
        print("Expected branches:", expected_branch_counts)
        print("Observed condition cell counts:", observed_cells_per_condition)
        print("Expected condition cell counts:", expected_cells_per_condition)
        # Explicit failure is useful for the fixed CALCE input series.
        # Disable only for a deliberately different input dataset.
        if os.environ.get("SOC_R22_ASSERT_MANUSCRIPT", "1") == "1":
            raise AssertionError(
                "R2.2 outputs differ from the frozen manuscript. "
                "Investigate inputs and processing; do not alter selection rules."
            )
    print("=" * 70)

"""R4A: Audit cycle-index imbalance and overlap in nominal-concordant CALCE cycles.

This metadata-only analysis does not filter/reconstruct cycles, fit voltage models,
or select an inferential analysis window. It reads the R2.2 global cycle store
and writes five descriptive CSV tables with the original R4A schemas.

Environment overrides:
    SOC_R22_DIR:  directory containing global_cycle_profiles.pkl
    SOC_OUTPUT_DIR: fallback to the same directory (STEP 3 convention)
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import os
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

REQUIRED_COLUMNS = [
    "sample_int", "Condition_ID", "Cycle_Index", "Temperature",
    "Rate", "Charge_Rate", "matches_nominal",
]
N_CONDITIONS = 24


def contiguous_windows(values):
    """Return inclusive (start, end, number_of_indices) consecutive runs."""
    indices = sorted({int(value) for value in values})
    if not indices:
        return []
    result = []
    start = previous = indices[0]
    for current in indices[1:]:
        if current != previous + 1:
            result.append((start, previous, previous - start + 1))
            start = current
        previous = current
    result.append((start, previous, previous - start + 1))
    return result


def window_table(coverage):
    """Preserve the original three common-index criteria and CSV column names."""
    criteria = {
        "all_24_conditions": "all_24_conditions",
        "all24_min_2_cells": "all24_with_min2cells",
        "all24_min_3_cells": "all24_with_min3cells",
    }
    records = []
    for label, column in criteria.items():
        for start, end, width in contiguous_windows(
            coverage.loc[coverage[column], "Cycle_Index"]
        ):
            records.append({
                "criterion": label,
                "start_cycle": start,
                "end_cycle": end,
                "width_cycles": width,
            })
    columns = ["criterion", "start_cycle", "end_cycle", "width_cycles"]
    result = pd.DataFrame.from_records(records, columns=columns)
    if not result.empty:
        result = result.sort_values(
            ["criterion", "width_cycles"], ascending=[True, False]
        ).reset_index(drop=True)
    return result


def run_age_cycle_audit(cycle_file: Path, output_dir: Path):
    """Return the five QA tables and save them without changing R2.2 inputs."""
    cycle_file = Path(cycle_file)
    output_dir = Path(output_dir)
    if not cycle_file.is_file():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"R2.2 global cycle store not found: {cycle_file}")
    output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    # Pickle loads the whole table; immediately discard unused voltage columns.
    source = pd.read_pickle(cycle_file)
    missing = [name for name in REQUIRED_COLUMNS if name not in source.columns]
    if missing:
        raise KeyError(f"R2.2 global store is missing columns: {', '.join(missing)}")
    d = source.loc[source["matches_nominal"].eq(True), REQUIRED_COLUMNS].copy()
    del source
    if d.empty:
        raise ValueError("R2.2 contains no nominal-concordant cycles")

    for name in ("sample_int", "Condition_ID", "Cycle_Index"):
        numeric = pd.to_numeric(d[name], errors="coerce")
        if numeric.isna().any() or (numeric % 1 != 0).any():
            raise ValueError(f"Invalid or nonintegral nominal metadata: {name}")
        d[name] = numeric.astype("int32")

    if d["Condition_ID"].nunique() != N_CONDITIONS:
        raise ValueError("Expected the full 24-condition nominal-concordant design")
    if not d["Condition_ID"].between(1, N_CONDITIONS).all():
        raise ValueError("Condition IDs must be 1 through 24")

    cycle_key = ["sample_int", "Condition_ID", "Cycle_Index"]
    if d.duplicated(subset=cycle_key).any():
        raise ValueError("Duplicate physical-cell/condition/cycle keys in R2.2 store")

    print(f"Nominal-concordant cycles: {len(d):,}")
    print(f"Cells: {d['sample_int'].nunique()}")
    print(f"Conditions: {d['Condition_ID'].nunique()}")

    # Quantiles are calculated across cycle observations, as in original R4A.
    condition_age = (
        d.groupby(
            ["Condition_ID", "Temperature", "Rate", "Charge_Rate"],
            as_index=False, observed=True,
        )
        .agg(
            n_cycles=("Cycle_Index", "size"),
            n_cells=("sample_int", "nunique"),
            cycle_min=("Cycle_Index", "min"),
            cycle_q05=("Cycle_Index", lambda x: float(np.quantile(x, 0.05))),
            cycle_q25=("Cycle_Index", lambda x: float(np.quantile(x, 0.25))),
            cycle_median=("Cycle_Index", "median"),
            cycle_q75=("Cycle_Index", lambda x: float(np.quantile(x, 0.75))),
            cycle_q95=("Cycle_Index", lambda x: float(np.quantile(x, 0.95))),
            cycle_max=("Cycle_Index", "max"),
        )
        .sort_values("Condition_ID")
        .reset_index(drop=True)
    )

    cell_age = d.groupby(
        ["Condition_ID", "sample_int"], as_index=False, observed=True
    ).agg(
        n_cycles=("Cycle_Index", "nunique"),
        cycle_min=("Cycle_Index", "min"),
        cycle_q25=("Cycle_Index", lambda x: float(np.quantile(x, 0.25))),
        cycle_median=("Cycle_Index", "median"),
        cycle_q75=("Cycle_Index", lambda x: float(np.quantile(x, 0.75))),
        cycle_max=("Cycle_Index", "max"),
    )

    lower = int(condition_age["cycle_min"].max())
    upper = int(condition_age["cycle_max"].min())

    # Equivalent to the original cycle-by-cycle filter, without 611 rescans.
    count_by_cycle_condition = (
        d.groupby(["Cycle_Index", "Condition_ID"], observed=True)["sample_int"]
        .nunique()
        .rename("n_cells")
        .reset_index()
    )
    group_coverage = count_by_cycle_condition.groupby(
        "Cycle_Index", as_index=False, observed=True
    ).agg(
        n_conditions=("Condition_ID", "nunique"),
        min_cells_per_present_condition=("n_cells", "min"),
        median_cells_per_present_condition=("n_cells", "median"),
    )
    # Retain the exact original columns and semantics.
    coverage = group_coverage[["Cycle_Index", "n_conditions"]].copy()
    coverage["all_24_conditions"] = coverage["n_conditions"].eq(N_CONDITIONS)
    coverage["min_cells_per_present_condition"] = group_coverage[
        "min_cells_per_present_condition"
    ]
    coverage["median_cells_per_present_condition"] = group_coverage[
        "median_cells_per_present_condition"
    ]
    coverage["all24_with_min2cells"] = (
        coverage["all_24_conditions"]
        & coverage["min_cells_per_present_condition"].ge(2)
    )
    coverage["all24_with_min3cells"] = (
        coverage["all_24_conditions"]
        & coverage["min_cells_per_present_condition"].ge(3)
    )
    windows = window_table(coverage)

    # Preserve the original unordered-pair definition (24 choose 2 = 276).
    pairs = []
    for i in range(len(condition_age)):
        a = condition_age.iloc[i]
        for j in range(i + 1, len(condition_age)):
            b = condition_age.iloc[j]
            pairs.append({
                "Condition_A": int(a["Condition_ID"]),
                "Condition_B": int(b["Condition_ID"]),
                "median_cycle_A": float(a["cycle_median"]),
                "median_cycle_B": float(b["cycle_median"]),
                "abs_median_cycle_difference": abs(
                    float(a["cycle_median"]) - float(b["cycle_median"])
                ),
            })
    pair_age = pd.DataFrame(pairs).sort_values(
        "abs_median_cycle_difference", ascending=False
    ).reset_index(drop=True)

    output_tables = {
        "condition_cycle_age_distribution.csv": condition_age,
        "cell_cycle_age_distribution.csv": cell_age,
        "cycle_index_condition_coverage.csv": coverage,
        "common_cycle_windows.csv": windows,
        "pairwise_condition_age_difference.csv": pair_age,
    }
    for filename, table in output_tables.items():
        table.to_csv(output_dir / filename, index=False)

    print("\n=== CONDITION-LEVEL CYCLE DISTRIBUTIONS ===")
    print(condition_age.to_string(index=False))
    print("\n=== SIMPLE AGE-RANGE INTERSECTION ===")
    print(f"max(condition minimum) = {lower}")
    print(f"min(condition maximum) = {upper}")
    if lower <= upper:
        print(f"Common min/max range: {lower}–{upper}")
        print(f"Width: {upper - lower + 1} cycle indices")
    else:
        print("No common min/max range across the 24 conditions")

    print("\n=== LONGEST COMMON-CYCLE WINDOWS ===")
    for criterion in [
        "all_24_conditions", "all24_min_2_cells", "all24_min_3_cells"
    ]:
        part = windows.loc[windows["criterion"].eq(criterion)]
        print(f"\n{criterion}")
        print(part.head(10).to_string(index=False) if not part.empty else "  NONE")

    low = float(condition_age["cycle_median"].min())
    high = float(condition_age["cycle_median"].max())
    print("\n=== CONDITION MEDIAN-AGE SPREAD ===")
    print(f"Lowest condition median cycle: {low}")
    print(f"Highest condition median cycle: {high}")
    print(f"Range: {high - low}")
    print("\n=== TOP 20 CONDITION AGE SEPARATIONS ===")
    print(pair_age.head(20).to_string(index=False))
    print("\n" + "=" * 72)
    print("R4A AGE/CYCLE OVERLAP AUDIT COMPLETE")
    print("=" * 72)
    print(f"Output directory:\n{output_dir}")
    print("Note: exact-index cell counts are descriptive; they do not replace")
    print("cell-level bracketing or SoC-band coverage in later inference.")
    return output_tables


def main():
    default_dir = (
        Path("/content/revision_R2_2_global")
        if Path("/content").is_dir()
        else Path("outputs/revision_R2_2_global")
    )
    r22_dir = Path(os.getenv("SOC_R22_DIR", os.getenv("SOC_OUTPUT_DIR", str(default_dir))))
    run_age_cycle_audit(
        cycle_file=r22_dir / "global_cycle_profiles.pkl",
        output_dir=r22_dir / "R4_age_cycle",
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""CALCE V-SoC | R4B (clean): 52-132 cycle-window cell and condition profiles.

Input: global_cycle_profiles.pkl (created by R2.2).
GitHub: scripts/build_window_cell_profiles.py

Run (from repository root):
    python scripts/build_window_cell_profiles.py

Optional paths (also work in Google Colab):
    VSOC_GLOBAL_CYCLES=/path/to/global_cycle_profiles.pkl
    VSOC_OUTPUT_DIR=/path/to/output

Scientific protocol (unchanged from manuscript-aligned R4B):
  nominal-concordant cycles -> [52, 132] inclusive -> pointwise median
  within each cell -> pointwise median across physical cells.
  Retains all 101 SoC output nodes; unsupported nodes remain NaN.
  Band-level 80% eligibility and inferential modelling happen downstream.
  QA counts are explicit and intentionally tied to manuscript inputs.
"""

from pathlib import Path
import os

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd


# ======================= CONFIGURATION =======================
WINDOW_START = 52                    # inclusive
WINDOW_END = 132                     # inclusive
ANALYSIS_NAME = "broad_52_132"
SOC_GRID = np.arange(0, 101, dtype=float)
V_COLS = [f"V_{i:03d}" for i in range(101)]

# Verified counts from the current paper's R2.2/R4B run.
# Stop rather than publish silently altered source data or output.
EXPECTED = {
    "nominal_cycles": 83_045,
    "nominal_cells": 180,
    "nominal_conditions": 24,
    "window_cycles": 13_181,
    "window_cells": 179,
    "window_conditions": 24,
}


def resolve_input():
    explicit = os.environ.get("VSOC_GLOBAL_CYCLES")
    if explicit:
        path = Path(explicit).expanduser()
        if not path.is_file():
            raise FileNotFoundError(f"VSOC_GLOBAL_CYCLES input not found: {path}")
        return path

    candidates = [
        Path("data/interim/global_cycle_profiles.pkl"),
        Path("global_cycle_profiles.pkl"),
        Path("/content/revision_R2_2_global/global_cycle_profiles.pkl"),
    ]
    for path in candidates:
        if path.is_file():
            return path
    raise FileNotFoundError(
        "R2.2 global_cycle_profiles.pkl not found. "
        "Place it under data/interim/ or set VSOC_GLOBAL_CYCLES."
    )


def require_equal(label, actual, expected):
    if actual != expected:
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"QA FAILED: {label}: observed={actual:,}, expected={expected:,}. "
            "Check the R2.2 input before publication."
        )


def build_profiles(cycle):
    required = [
        "matches_nominal", "sample_int", "Condition_ID", "Cycle_Index",
        "Temperature", "Rate", "Charge_Rate", *V_COLS,
    ]
    missing = [c for c in required if c not in cycle.columns]
    if missing:
        raise KeyError(f"Missing columns in R2.2 global cycle store: {missing}")

    nominal = cycle.loc[cycle["matches_nominal"] == True].copy()
    require_equal("Nominal cycles", len(nominal), EXPECTED["nominal_cycles"])
    require_equal("Nominal cells", nominal["sample_int"].nunique(), EXPECTED["nominal_cells"])
    require_equal("Nominal conditions", nominal["Condition_ID"].nunique(), EXPECTED["nominal_conditions"])

    keys = ["sample_int", "Condition_ID", "Cycle_Index"]
    if nominal[keys].isna().any().any() or nominal.duplicated(keys).any():
        raise ValueError("Missing or duplicate physical cycle keys in nominal branch.")

    metadata_cols = ["Condition_ID", "Temperature", "Rate", "Charge_Rate"]
    condition_meta = nominal[metadata_cols].drop_duplicates()
    if condition_meta["Condition_ID"].duplicated().any():
        raise ValueError("Conflicting condition metadata for the same Condition_ID.")

    # R4B original inclusive window: Cycle_Index >= 52 AND <= 132.
    window = nominal.loc[
        nominal["Cycle_Index"].between(WINDOW_START, WINDOW_END, inclusive="both")
    ].copy()
    require_equal("Window cycles", len(window), EXPECTED["window_cycles"])
    require_equal("Window cells", window["sample_int"].nunique(), EXPECTED["window_cells"])
    require_equal("Window conditions", window["Condition_ID"].nunique(), EXPECTED["window_conditions"])

    # Original R4B: distinct cycle count per physical cell and condition.
    cell_support = window.groupby(
        ["Condition_ID", "sample_int"], as_index=False, observed=True
    ).agg(
        n_cycles=("Cycle_Index", "nunique"),
        cycle_min=("Cycle_Index", "min"),
        cycle_median=("Cycle_Index", "median"),
        cycle_max=("Cycle_Index", "max"),
    )
    support = cell_support.groupby(
        "Condition_ID", as_index=False, observed=True
    ).agg(
        n_cells=("sample_int", "nunique"),
        total_cycles=("n_cycles", "sum"),
        min_cycles_per_cell=("n_cycles", "min"),
        median_cycles_per_cell=("n_cycles", "median"),
        max_cycles_per_cell=("n_cycles", "max"),
    )
    support["analysis"] = ANALYSIS_NAME

    # Original R4B calculation: for each cell, median over its cycles
    # independently at each SoC node, with node-specific cycle support.
    cell_rows = []
    for (cid, sid), g in window.groupby(
        ["Condition_ID", "sample_int"], observed=True, sort=False
    ):
        A = g[V_COLS].to_numpy(dtype=float)
        if np.isinf(A).any():
            raise ValueError(
                f"Nonfinite infinite voltage in condition={cid}, cell={sid}."
            )

        # Preserve unsupported SoC nodes as NaN. Compute each median only
        # on columns with finite cycle observations; this avoids spurious
        # NumPy All-NaN warnings without changing finite-node estimates.
        n = np.isfinite(A).sum(axis=0)
        supported = n > 0
        med = np.full(len(V_COLS), np.nan, dtype=float)
        if supported.any():
            med[supported] = np.nanmedian(A[:, supported], axis=0)
        for j, soc in enumerate(SOC_GRID):
            cell_rows.append({
                "analysis": ANALYSIS_NAME,
                "Condition_ID": int(cid),
                "sample_int": int(sid),
                "SoC": float(soc),
                "Voltage": float(med[j]) if np.isfinite(med[j]) else np.nan,
                "n_cycles_at_soc": int(n[j]),
            })
    cell_profiles = pd.DataFrame(cell_rows)

    # Original R4B calculation: equal weight per physical cell.
    condition_profiles = (
        cell_profiles.groupby(
            ["Condition_ID", "SoC"], as_index=False, observed=True
        ).agg(
            Voltage=("Voltage", "median"),
            n_cells_at_soc=("Voltage", lambda z: int(np.isfinite(z).sum())),
        ).merge(condition_meta, on="Condition_ID", how="left", validate="many_to_one")
    )
    condition_profiles["analysis"] = ANALYSIS_NAME

    require_equal("Cell-profile rows", len(cell_profiles), EXPECTED["window_cells"] * len(SOC_GRID))
    require_equal("Condition-profile rows", len(condition_profiles), EXPECTED["window_conditions"] * len(SOC_GRID))
    require_equal("Support-cycle total", int(support["total_cycles"].sum()), len(window))
    if (cell_profiles.loc[cell_profiles["n_cycles_at_soc"] == 0, "Voltage"].notna()).any():
        raise RuntimeError("Voltage found at a zero-cycle-support SoC node.")
    return cell_profiles, condition_profiles, support


def main():
    input_file = resolve_input()
    output_dir = Path(os.environ.get("VSOC_OUTPUT_DIR", "outputs/R4B_broad_52_132")).expanduser()
    print(f"Input: {input_file}")
    cycle = pd.read_pickle(input_file)  # trusted, locally generated R2.2 pickle only
    cell_profiles, condition_profiles, support = build_profiles(cycle)
    output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    outputs = {
        "broad_52_132_cell_profiles.csv": cell_profiles,
        "broad_52_132_condition_profiles.csv": condition_profiles,
        "broad_52_132_condition_support.csv": support,
    }
    for filename, frame in outputs.items():
        path = output_dir / filename
        frame.to_csv(path, index=False)
        print(f"Saved: {path}  ({len(frame):,} rows)")
    print("\nR4B 52–132 QA PASSED")
    print(f"Window: {WINDOW_START}–{WINDOW_END} (inclusive)")
    print(f"Cycles: {int(support['total_cycles'].sum()):,}")
    print(f"Cells: {int(support['n_cells'].sum()):,}")
    print(f"Conditions: {support['Condition_ID'].nunique():,}")
    print(f"Cell-profile rows: {len(cell_profiles):,}")
    print(f"Condition-profile rows: {len(condition_profiles):,}")
    print("\nCONDITION SUPPORT")
    print(support.to_string(index=False))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""R9: condition-specific voltage--SoC LUT and technical QA.

Scientific protocol preserved from the historical R9 script:
- R2.2 nominal-concordant cell profiles, all cells and equal cell weights
- pointwise median across physical cells, weighted isotonic regression
  using the number of supporting cells at each SoC node
- 0..100 integer SoC grid, no anchor, no guard, no extrapolation
- internal unsupported nodes CAN receive interpolated isotonic estimates;
  this is why R9 must not substitute for the final unified validation LUT.

R9 is retained as a diagnostic/technical historical stage. It is not the
canonical source for final manuscript LOCO, rate or temperature validation.

Inputs:  cell_profiles_nominal_concordant.csv (R2.2)
Outputs: original nine R9 files, with original names and column schemas.
"""

from pathlib import Path
import os
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from sklearn.isotonic import IsotonicRegression

SOC_GRID = np.arange(0, 101, dtype=float)
MONO_TOL_V = 1e-9
CORRECTION_THRESHOLDS_MV = (1, 2, 5, 10)
EXPECTED_CONDITIONS = 24
REGIONS = (("0-90", 0, 90), ("90-100", 90, 100.0001),
           ("95-100", 95, 100.0001))


def resolve_input():
    explicit = os.environ.get("VSOC_R9_CELL_FILE")
    if explicit:
        p = Path(explicit).expanduser()
        if not p.is_file():
            raise FileNotFoundError(f"R9 input file not found: {p}")
        return p
    for p in (
        Path("data/interim/cell_profiles_nominal_concordant.csv"),
        Path("cell_profiles_nominal_concordant.csv"),
        Path("/content/revision_R2_2_global/cell_profiles_nominal_concordant.csv"),
    ):
        if p.is_file():
            return p
    raise FileNotFoundError(
        "Missing R2.2 cell_profiles_nominal_concordant.csv. "
        "Set VSOC_R9_CELL_FILE to its path."
    )


def resolve_output(input_file):
    explicit = os.environ.get("VSOC_R9_OUTPUT_DIR")
    if explicit:
        return Path(explicit).expanduser()
    if str(input_file).startswith("/content/"):
        return Path("/content/revision_R2_2_global/R9_final_LUT")
    return Path("outputs/R9_final_LUT")


def _finite_quantile(values, quantile):
    a = np.asarray(values, dtype=float)
    return float(np.nanquantile(a, quantile)) if np.isfinite(a).any() else np.nan


def _finite_count(values):
    return int(np.isfinite(np.asarray(values, dtype=float)).sum())


def load_cell_profiles(path):
    d = pd.read_csv(path)
    required = ["sample_int", "Condition_ID", "Temperature", "Rate",
                "Charge_Rate", "SoC", "V_cell_median"]
    missing = [c for c in required if c not in d]
    if missing:
        raise KeyError(f"R9 input missing columns: {missing}")

    for c in ("sample_int", "Condition_ID", "Temperature", "SoC", "V_cell_median"):
        d[c] = pd.to_numeric(d[c], errors="coerce")
    # Preserve the original R9 treatment of missing identifiers.
    d = d.dropna(subset=["sample_int", "Condition_ID", "SoC"]).copy()
    d["sample_int"] = d["sample_int"].astype(int)
    d["Condition_ID"] = d["Condition_ID"].astype(int)
    return d


def prepare_raw(d):
    meta = (d[["Condition_ID", "Temperature", "Rate", "Charge_Rate"]]
            .drop_duplicates(subset=["Condition_ID"])
            .sort_values("Condition_ID"))
    nominal_n = (d[["Condition_ID", "sample_int"]].drop_duplicates()
                 .groupby("Condition_ID")["sample_int"].nunique().to_dict())

    raw = (d.groupby(["Condition_ID", "SoC"], as_index=False, observed=True)
           .agg(V_raw_median=("V_cell_median", "median"),
                V_q25=("V_cell_median", lambda x: _finite_quantile(x, 0.25)),
                V_q75=("V_cell_median", lambda x: _finite_quantile(x, 0.75)),
                n_cells_at_soc=("V_cell_median", _finite_count)))
    raw = raw.merge(meta, on="Condition_ID", how="left")
    raw["nominal_n_cells"] = raw["Condition_ID"].map(nominal_n)
    raw["support_fraction"] = raw["n_cells_at_soc"] / raw["nominal_n_cells"]
    raw["minimum_strong_support"] = np.ceil(raw["nominal_n_cells"] / 2.0).astype(int)
    raw["strong_support"] = raw["n_cells_at_soc"] >= raw["minimum_strong_support"]
    return raw, meta, nominal_n


def build_condition_luts(raw, meta, nominal_n):
    lut_rows, correction_rows, qa_rows = [], [], []
    for cid in range(1, EXPECTED_CONDITIONS + 1):
        q = raw.loc[raw["Condition_ID"] == cid].sort_values("SoC").copy()
        fitdata = q.loc[q["V_raw_median"].notna() & (q["n_cells_at_soc"] > 0)].copy()
        if len(fitdata) < 2:
            raise RuntimeError(f"Condition {cid}: insufficient observed SoC support.")
        x = fitdata["SoC"].to_numpy(float)
        y = fitdata["V_raw_median"].to_numpy(float)
        weights = fitdata["n_cells_at_soc"].to_numpy(float)
        dy_raw = np.diff(y)
        n_raw_violations = int(np.sum(dy_raw < -MONO_TOL_V))
        worst_raw_drop_V = float(np.min(dy_raw)) if len(dy_raw) else np.nan

        iso = IsotonicRegression(increasing=True, out_of_bounds="nan")
        iso.fit(x, y, sample_weight=weights)
        pred_grid = iso.predict(SOC_GRID)
        observed_min_soc, observed_max_soc = float(np.min(x)), float(np.max(x))
        pred_grid[(SOC_GRID < observed_min_soc) | (SOC_GRID > observed_max_soc)] = np.nan

        qgrid = pd.DataFrame({"SoC": SOC_GRID}).merge(
            q[["SoC", "V_raw_median", "V_q25", "V_q75",
               "n_cells_at_soc", "nominal_n_cells", "support_fraction", "strong_support"]],
            on="SoC", how="left"
        )
        qgrid["V_LUT"] = pred_grid
        qgrid["is_observed_node"] = qgrid["V_raw_median"].notna()
        qgrid["is_interpolated_node"] = (
            qgrid["V_raw_median"].isna() & qgrid["V_LUT"].notna()
        )
        qgrid["correction_V"] = qgrid["V_LUT"] - qgrid["V_raw_median"]
        qgrid["abs_correction_mV"] = np.abs(qgrid["correction_V"]) * 1000.0
        final_valid = qgrid["V_LUT"].dropna().to_numpy(float)
        final_violations = int(np.sum(np.diff(final_valid) < -MONO_TOL_V))
        observed_corrections = qgrid.loc[
            qgrid["V_raw_median"].notna() & qgrid["V_LUT"].notna(),
            "abs_correction_mV"
        ]
        n_missing_final = int(qgrid["V_LUT"].isna().sum())
        n_weak_support = int((
            qgrid["is_observed_node"] & (qgrid["strong_support"] != True)
        ).sum())
        meta_row = meta.loc[meta["Condition_ID"] == cid].iloc[0]
        qa = {
            "Condition_ID": cid,
            "Temperature": int(meta_row["Temperature"]),
            "Rate": str(meta_row["Rate"]),
            "Charge_Rate": str(meta_row["Charge_Rate"]),
            "nominal_n_cells": int(nominal_n[cid]),
            "observed_soc_min": observed_min_soc,
            "observed_soc_max": observed_max_soc,
            "n_observed_soc_nodes": int(qgrid["is_observed_node"].sum()),
            "n_interpolated_internal_nodes": int(qgrid["is_interpolated_node"].sum()),
            "n_missing_final_nodes": n_missing_final,
            "n_weak_support_nodes": n_weak_support,
            "raw_monotonicity_violations": n_raw_violations,
            "worst_raw_single_step_drop_mV": worst_raw_drop_V * 1000.0,
            "final_monotonicity_violations": final_violations,
            "mean_abs_isotonic_correction_mV": (
                float(observed_corrections.mean()) if len(observed_corrections) else np.nan
            ),
            "median_abs_isotonic_correction_mV": (
                float(observed_corrections.median()) if len(observed_corrections) else np.nan
            ),
            "max_abs_isotonic_correction_mV": (
                float(observed_corrections.max()) if len(observed_corrections) else np.nan
            ),
        }
        for threshold in CORRECTION_THRESHOLDS_MV:
            qa[f"n_corrections_gt_{threshold}mV"] = int((observed_corrections > threshold).sum())
        qa_rows.append(qa)

        for _, r in qgrid.iterrows():
            lut_rows.append({
                "Condition_ID": cid,
                "Temperature": int(meta_row["Temperature"]),
                "Rate": str(meta_row["Rate"]),
                "Charge_Rate": str(meta_row["Charge_Rate"]),
                "SoC": float(r["SoC"]),
                "V_raw_median": r["V_raw_median"],
                "V_LUT": r["V_LUT"],
                "n_cells_at_soc": r["n_cells_at_soc"],
                "nominal_n_cells": r["nominal_n_cells"],
                "support_fraction": r["support_fraction"],
                "strong_support": r["strong_support"],
                "is_observed_node": bool(r["is_observed_node"]),
                "is_interpolated_node": bool(r["is_interpolated_node"]),
                "correction_V": r["correction_V"],
                "abs_correction_mV": r["abs_correction_mV"],
            })
            if pd.notna(r["V_raw_median"]):
                correction_rows.append({
                    "Condition_ID": cid, "SoC": float(r["SoC"]),
                    "V_raw_median": float(r["V_raw_median"]),
                    "V_LUT": float(r["V_LUT"]),
                    "correction_mV": float(r["correction_V"] * 1000.0),
                    "abs_correction_mV": float(r["abs_correction_mV"]),
                    "n_cells_at_soc": int(r["n_cells_at_soc"]),
                })
    return pd.DataFrame(lut_rows), pd.DataFrame(correction_rows), pd.DataFrame(qa_rows)


def build_support_qa(lut):
    records = []
    # Preserve original R9 bin semantics: 0-90 => [0,90), 90-100 => [90,100].
    for cid, g in lut.groupby("Condition_ID", observed=True):
        for region, lo, hi in REGIONS:
            q = g.loc[(g["SoC"] >= lo) & (g["SoC"] < hi)]
            observed = q.loc[q["is_observed_node"] == True]
            if len(observed):
                min_support = int(observed["n_cells_at_soc"].min())
                median_support = float(observed["n_cells_at_soc"].median())
                min_fraction = float(observed["support_fraction"].min())
            else:
                min_support, median_support, min_fraction = 0, np.nan, np.nan
            records.append({
                "Condition_ID": int(cid), "SoC_region": region,
                "n_grid_nodes": int(len(q)),
                "n_observed_nodes": int(q["is_observed_node"].sum()),
                "n_LUT_nodes": int(q["V_LUT"].notna().sum()),
                "minimum_cells_support": min_support,
                "median_cells_support": median_support,
                "minimum_support_fraction": min_fraction,
                "n_weak_support_nodes": int((
                    q["is_observed_node"] & (q["strong_support"] != True)
                ).sum()),
            })
    return pd.DataFrame(records)


def export_results(lut, qa, corrections, support_qa, out):
    matrix = (lut.pivot(index="Condition_ID", columns="SoC", values="V_LUT")
              .reindex(index=range(1, 25), columns=SOC_GRID))
    matrix.columns = [f"SoC_{int(x):03d}" for x in matrix.columns]
    expected_values = 24 * 101
    actual_rows = len(lut)
    n_finite_lut = int(np.isfinite(lut["V_LUT"]).sum())
    all_monotone = bool((qa["final_monotonicity_violations"] == 0).all())
    complete_grid = actual_rows == expected_values
    complete_values = n_finite_lut == expected_values

    arr32 = matrix.to_numpy(dtype=np.float32)
    arr16 = matrix.to_numpy(dtype=np.float16)
    finite = np.isfinite(arr32) & np.isfinite(arr16)
    qerr = np.abs(arr32[finite] - arr16[finite].astype(np.float32))
    quantization = pd.DataFrame([{
        "n_values": int(finite.sum()),
        "float32_bytes": int(arr32.nbytes),
        "float16_bytes": int(arr16.nbytes),
        "float32_KiB": float(arr32.nbytes / 1024),
        "float16_KiB": float(arr16.nbytes / 1024),
        "float16_mean_abs_quantization_error_mV": (
            float(np.mean(qerr) * 1000) if len(qerr) else np.nan
        ),
        "float16_p95_abs_quantization_error_mV": (
            float(np.quantile(qerr, 0.95) * 1000) if len(qerr) else np.nan
        ),
        "float16_max_abs_quantization_error_mV": (
            float(np.max(qerr) * 1000) if len(qerr) else np.nan
        ),
    }])

    global_qa = pd.DataFrame([{
        "n_conditions": int(lut["Condition_ID"].nunique()),
        "expected_nodes": expected_values,
        "actual_rows": actual_rows,
        "finite_LUT_values": n_finite_lut,
        "complete_24x101_grid": complete_grid,
        "all_2424_values_finite": complete_values,
        "all_final_curves_monotone": all_monotone,
        "total_raw_monotonicity_violations": int(qa["raw_monotonicity_violations"].sum()),
        "total_final_monotonicity_violations": int(qa["final_monotonicity_violations"].sum()),
        "global_mean_abs_correction_mV": float(corrections["abs_correction_mV"].mean()),
        "global_median_abs_correction_mV": float(corrections["abs_correction_mV"].median()),
        "global_p95_abs_correction_mV": float(corrections["abs_correction_mV"].quantile(0.95)),
        "global_max_abs_correction_mV": float(corrections["abs_correction_mV"].max()),
    }])

    # As in the historical R9, enforce monotonicity and coordinate completeness.
    if not all_monotone:
        raise RuntimeError("FINAL QA FAILED: at least one LUT is non-monotone.")
    if not complete_grid:
        raise RuntimeError("FINAL QA FAILED: 24x101 row grid is incomplete.")

    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    lut.to_csv(out / "R9_final_LUT_long.csv", index=False)
    matrix.to_csv(out / "R9_final_LUT_matrix.csv")
    qa.to_csv(out / "R9_condition_QA.csv", index=False)
    support_qa.to_csv(out / "R9_SoC_support_QA.csv", index=False)
    corrections.to_csv(out / "R9_monotonic_corrections.csv", index=False)
    quantization.to_csv(out / "R9_quantization_QA.csv", index=False)
    global_qa.to_csv(out / "R9_global_QA.csv", index=False)
    np.save(out / "R9_final_LUT_float32.npy", arr32)
    np.save(out / "R9_final_LUT_float16.npy", arr16)

    print("\nExpected LUT nodes:", expected_values)
    print("Actual LUT rows:", actual_rows)
    print("Finite LUT values:", n_finite_lut)
    print("All final curves monotone:", all_monotone)
    print("\n" + "=" * 80)
    print("R9 FINAL LUT + QA COMPLETE")
    print("=" * 80)
    print("\n=== GLOBAL LUT QA ===\n", global_qa.to_string(index=False))
    print("\n=== CONDITION QA ===\n", qa.to_string(index=False))
    print("\n=== 90–100% SUPPORT QA ===\n",
          support_qa[support_qa["SoC_region"] == "90-100"].to_string(index=False))
    print("\n=== FLOAT16 / FLOAT32 QUANTIZATION QA ===\n",
          quantization.to_string(index=False))
    print("\n=== LARGEST 25 ISOTONIC CORRECTIONS ===\n",
          corrections.sort_values("abs_correction_mV", ascending=False).head(25).to_string(index=False))
    if not complete_values:
        print("\nWARNING: The 24×101 coordinate grid includes unsupported NaN "
              "endpoints. DO NOT extrapolate them silently.")
    print("\nOutput directory:\n", out)


def main():
    input_path = resolve_input()
    out = resolve_output(input_path)
    d = load_cell_profiles(input_path)
    print("Input:", input_path)
    print("Physical cells:", d["sample_int"].nunique())
    print("Conditions:", d["Condition_ID"].nunique())
    print("Cell-condition pairs:",
          d[["sample_int", "Condition_ID"]].drop_duplicates().shape[0])
    raw, meta, nominal_n = prepare_raw(d)
    lut, corrections, qa = build_condition_luts(raw, meta, nominal_n)
    support_qa = build_support_qa(lut)
    export_results(lut, qa, corrections, support_qa, out)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""R10 | Package the historical R9 LUT on the common 0..90% SoC domain.

No model is fitted here. Scientific operations from the original R10 are
preserved: exact R9 0..90 inclusive extraction, 24x91 matrix, float32/float16
quantization, scalar/vectorized linear lookup, condition support diagnostics,
optional R5/R6/R7/R8 audit entries, and the ten original output artifacts.

R9/R10 are technical packaging/QA stages. Final manuscript LOCO, holdout, and
other inferential results must be reproduced using the later unified estimator.

Configuration:
    VSOC_R10_ROOT       Root directory holding R9_final_LUT/, R5_... etc.
    VSOC_R10_OUTPUT_DIR Optional export directory.
    VSOC_R10_SCALAR_BENCH, VSOC_R10_VECTOR_BENCH  Optional benchmark sizes.

Run: python R10_final_computational_audit_clean.py
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import os
import platform
import time
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

SOC_MIN, SOC_MAX = 0, 90
N_CONDITIONS, N_SOC = 24, 91
EXPECTED_VALUES = N_CONDITIONS * N_SOC
RNG_SEED = 20260924
MONO_TOL_V = 1e-9
DEFAULT_SCALAR_BENCH = 250_000
DEFAULT_VECTOR_BENCH = 2_000_000


def resolve_root() -> Path:
    explicit = os.environ.get("VSOC_R10_ROOT")
    if explicit:
        root = Path(explicit).expanduser()
        if not (root / "R9_final_LUT" / "R9_final_LUT_long.csv").is_file():
            raise FileNotFoundError(f"Missing R9 LUT under VSOC_R10_ROOT: {root}")
        return root
    candidates = (
        Path("."), Path("outputs"),
        Path("/content/revision_R2_2_global"),
    )
    for root in candidates:
        if (root / "R9_final_LUT" / "R9_final_LUT_long.csv").is_file():
            return root
    raise FileNotFoundError("R9_final_LUT_long.csv not found. Set VSOC_R10_ROOT.")


def require_columns(frame: pd.DataFrame, columns: tuple[str, ...] | list[str], name: str) -> None:
    missing = [col for col in columns if col not in frame]
    if missing:
        raise KeyError(f"{name} missing columns: {missing}")


def load_canonical(path: Path) -> pd.DataFrame:
    lut = pd.read_csv(path)
    needed = ("Condition_ID", "SoC", "V_LUT", "n_cells_at_soc", "support_fraction")
    require_columns(lut, needed, "R9 final LUT")
    for col in needed:
        lut[col] = pd.to_numeric(lut[col], errors="coerce")
    canonical = (lut.loc[lut["SoC"].between(SOC_MIN, SOC_MAX, inclusive="both")]
                 .sort_values(["Condition_ID", "SoC"]).reset_index(drop=True).copy())
    if canonical[["Condition_ID", "SoC"]].isna().any().any():
        raise ValueError("Canonical LUT contains missing condition/SoC keys")
    if canonical.duplicated(["Condition_ID", "SoC"]).any():
        raise ValueError("Canonical LUT contains duplicate condition/SoC keys")
    if len(canonical) != EXPECTED_VALUES:
        raise RuntimeError(f"Canonical R10 has {len(canonical)} rows; expected {EXPECTED_VALUES}")
    return canonical


def prepare_lut(canonical: pd.DataFrame):
    mono_rows = []
    for cid, g in canonical.groupby("Condition_ID", observed=True):
        v = g.sort_values("SoC")["V_LUT"].to_numpy(dtype=float)
        dv = np.diff(v)
        mono_rows.append({
            "Condition_ID": int(cid),
            "n_nodes": int(len(g)),
            "n_finite": int(np.isfinite(v).sum()),
            "monotonicity_violations": int((dv < -MONO_TOL_V).sum()),
            "minimum_dV_per_1pct_mV": float(np.min(dv) * 1000) if len(dv) else np.nan,
            "maximum_dV_per_1pct_mV": float(np.max(dv) * 1000) if len(dv) else np.nan,
        })
    mono = pd.DataFrame(mono_rows)
    grid_counts = canonical.groupby("Condition_ID")["SoC"].nunique()
    all_conditions_91 = bool(len(grid_counts) == N_CONDITIONS and (grid_counts == N_SOC).all())
    n_finite = int(np.isfinite(canonical["V_LUT"]).sum())
    all_finite = bool(n_finite == EXPECTED_VALUES)
    all_monotone = bool((mono["monotonicity_violations"] == 0).all())

    matrix = (canonical.pivot(index="Condition_ID", columns="SoC", values="V_LUT")
              .reindex(index=range(1, N_CONDITIONS + 1),
                       columns=np.arange(SOC_MIN, SOC_MAX + 1, dtype=float)))
    matrix.columns = [f"SoC_{i:03d}" for i in range(N_SOC)]
    arr64 = matrix.to_numpy(dtype=np.float64)
    arr32 = matrix.to_numpy(dtype=np.float32)
    arr16 = matrix.to_numpy(dtype=np.float16)
    if not all_conditions_91 or arr32.shape != (N_CONDITIONS, N_SOC):
        raise RuntimeError("R10 requires exactly 24 conditions with each 0..90% node")
    if not all_finite or not np.isfinite(arr32).all():
        raise RuntimeError("The canonical 0..90 LUT has unsupported or invalid nodes")
    if not all_monotone:
        raise RuntimeError("The canonical 0..90 LUT violates monotonicity")
    return matrix, arr64, arr32, arr16, mono, n_finite, all_monotone


def quantization_qa(arr64: np.ndarray, arr32: np.ndarray, arr16: np.ndarray):
    qerr = np.abs(arr32 - arr16.astype(np.float32))
    memory_quant = pd.DataFrame([{
        "n_conditions": N_CONDITIONS,
        "n_soc_nodes": N_SOC,
        "n_values": int(arr32.size),
        "float64_bytes": int(arr64.nbytes),
        "float32_bytes": int(arr32.nbytes),
        "float16_bytes": int(arr16.nbytes),
        "float64_KiB": float(arr64.nbytes / 1024),
        "float32_KiB": float(arr32.nbytes / 1024),
        "float16_KiB": float(arr16.nbytes / 1024),
        "float16_mean_abs_error_mV": float(np.mean(qerr) * 1000),
        "float16_p95_abs_error_mV": float(np.quantile(qerr, 0.95) * 1000),
        "float16_max_abs_error_mV": float(np.max(qerr) * 1000),
    }])
    return qerr, memory_quant


def lookup_scalar(table: np.ndarray, condition_id: int, soc: float) -> float:
    """Original two-point 1%-node interpolation; NaN outside the SoC domain."""
    cid = int(condition_id) - 1
    if not 0 <= cid < N_CONDITIONS:
        raise ValueError("condition_id must be 1...24")
    s = float(soc)
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < SOC_MIN or s > SOC_MAX:
        return np.nan
    lo = int(np.floor(s))
    if lo >= SOC_MAX:
        return float(table[cid, SOC_MAX])
    hi = lo + 1
    w = s - lo
    return float((1.0 - w) * table[cid, lo] + w * table[cid, hi])


def lookup_vectorized(table: np.ndarray, condition_ids, soc) -> np.ndarray:
    """Original vectorized 1%-node linear interpolation, float32 output."""
    condition_ids = np.asarray(condition_ids, dtype=np.int32)
    soc = np.asarray(soc, dtype=np.float32)
    if condition_ids.ndim != 1 or soc.ndim != 1 or len(condition_ids) != len(soc):
        raise ValueError("Condition and SoC inputs must be equal-length 1D vectors")
    out = np.full(len(soc), np.nan, dtype=np.float32)
    valid = ((condition_ids >= 1) & (condition_ids <= N_CONDITIONS)
             & (soc >= SOC_MIN) & (soc <= SOC_MAX))
    if not valid.any():
        return out
    cid = condition_ids[valid] - 1
    sv = soc[valid]
    lo = np.floor(sv).astype(np.int32)
    hi = np.minimum(lo + 1, SOC_MAX)
    w = (sv - lo).astype(np.float32)
    out[valid] = ((1.0 - w) * table[cid, lo] + w * table[cid, hi])
    return out


def check_lookup_consistency(arr32: np.ndarray, rng: np.random.Generator) -> float:
    ids = rng.integers(1, 25, size=10_000, dtype=np.int32)
    socs = rng.uniform(0, 90, size=10_000).astype(np.float32)
    vec = lookup_vectorized(arr32, ids, socs)
    sca = np.array([lookup_scalar(arr32, c, s) for c, s in zip(ids, socs)],
                   dtype=np.float32)
    return float(np.max(np.abs(vec - sca)) * 1e6)


def run_benchmark(arr32: np.ndarray, rng: np.random.Generator):
    scalar_n = int(os.environ.get("VSOC_R10_SCALAR_BENCH", DEFAULT_SCALAR_BENCH))
    vector_n = int(os.environ.get("VSOC_R10_VECTOR_BENCH", DEFAULT_VECTOR_BENCH))
    if scalar_n < 1_000 or vector_n < 10_000:
        raise ValueError("R10 benchmark needs >=1,000 scalar and >=10,000 vector lookups")
    bench_rows = []
    scalar_ids = rng.integers(1, 25, size=scalar_n)
    scalar_soc = rng.uniform(0, 90, size=scalar_n)
    for i in range(1_000):
        lookup_scalar(arr32, scalar_ids[i], scalar_soc[i])
    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    checksum_scalar = 0.0
    for cid, soc in zip(scalar_ids, scalar_soc):
        checksum_scalar += lookup_scalar(arr32, cid, soc)
    scalar_seconds = time.perf_counter() - t0
    bench_rows.append({
        "benchmark": "scalar_python_float32", "n_lookups": scalar_n,
        "elapsed_seconds": scalar_seconds,
        "lookups_per_second": scalar_n / scalar_seconds,
        "mean_time_per_lookup_us": scalar_seconds / scalar_n * 1e6,
        "checksum": checksum_scalar,
    })
    vector_ids = rng.integers(1, 25, size=vector_n, dtype=np.int32)
    vector_soc = rng.uniform(0, 90, size=vector_n).astype(np.float32)
    _ = lookup_vectorized(arr32, vector_ids[:10_000], vector_soc[:10_000])
    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    vec = lookup_vectorized(arr32, vector_ids, vector_soc)
    vector_seconds = time.perf_counter() - t0
    bench_rows.append({
        "benchmark": "vectorized_numpy_float32", "n_lookups": vector_n,
        "elapsed_seconds": vector_seconds,
        "lookups_per_second": vector_n / vector_seconds,
        "mean_time_per_lookup_us": vector_seconds / vector_n * 1e6,
        "checksum": float(np.nansum(vec)),
    })
    benchmark = pd.DataFrame(bench_rows)
    benchmark["python_version"] = platform.python_version()
    benchmark["platform"] = platform.platform()
    return benchmark


def condition_support(canonical: pd.DataFrame):
    return (canonical.groupby("Condition_ID", as_index=False, observed=True)
            .agg(n_nodes=("SoC", "size"),
                 minimum_cell_support=("n_cells_at_soc", "min"),
                 median_cell_support=("n_cells_at_soc", "median"),
                 minimum_support_fraction=("support_fraction", "min"),
                 n_nodes_support_1=("n_cells_at_soc", lambda x: int(np.sum(np.asarray(x) == 1))),
                 n_nodes_support_lt_half=("support_fraction", lambda x: int(np.sum(np.asarray(x) < 0.5)))))


def build_audit(root: Path, arr32: np.ndarray, mono: pd.DataFrame,
                consistency_uV: float, n_finite: int):
    audit_rows = []
    def record(item, status, value, criterion, note=""):
        audit_rows.append({"item": item, "status": status, "value": value,
                           "criterion": criterion, "note": note})

    record("canonical_LUT_shape", "PASS" if arr32.shape == (24, 91) else "FAIL",
           str(arr32.shape), "(24, 91)")
    record("canonical_LUT_finite", "PASS" if n_finite == EXPECTED_VALUES else "FAIL",
           f"{n_finite}/{EXPECTED_VALUES}", "2184/2184 finite")
    record("canonical_LUT_monotone",
           "PASS" if (mono["monotonicity_violations"] == 0).all() else "FAIL",
           int(mono["monotonicity_violations"].sum()), "0 violations")
    record("scalar_vector_lookup_consistency",
           "PASS" if consistency_uV < 1.0 else "REVIEW", consistency_uV,
           "<1 microvolt numerical difference")

    r9qa_path = root / "R9_final_LUT" / "R9_condition_QA.csv"
    r9qa = pd.read_csv(r9qa_path)
    require_columns(r9qa, ["n_missing_final_nodes"], "R9_condition_QA")
    r9_missing = int(r9qa["n_missing_final_nodes"].sum())
    record("common_0_100_LUT_support", "REVIEW", r9_missing,
           "0 missing would be required for full 0–100 common LUT",
           "Full 0–100 common grid is not supported; final common domain restricted to 0–90.")

    # Optional historical comparisons only. They do not validate/re-fit the
    # canonical LUT, and absent source files produce no fabricated PASS entries.
    r5_path = root / "R5_out_of_sample_validation" / "R5_validation_summary.csv"
    if r5_path.exists():
        r5 = pd.read_csv(r5_path)
        require_columns(r5, ["analysis", "SoC_band", "validation", "mean_MAE_mV"], "R5 summary")
        main = r5.loc[(r5["analysis"] == "broad_52_132") & (r5["SoC_band"] == "0-90")]
        for validation in ("leave_one_cell_out", "discharge_rate_level_holdout",
                           "temperature_level_holdout"):
            q = main.loc[main["validation"] == validation]
            if len(q):
                record(f"R5_{validation}_MAE_mV", "PASS", float(q.iloc[0]["mean_MAE_mV"]),
                       "reported descriptive OOS result", "No pass/fail accuracy threshold imposed.")

    r61_path = (root / "R6_high_soc_guard" / "R6_1_unguarded_comparison"
                / "unguarded_vs_guarded_summary.csv")
    if r61_path.exists():
        r61 = pd.read_csv(r61_path)
        require_columns(r61, ["analysis", "SoC_band", "method", "mean_MAE_mV"], "R6.1 summary")
        high = r61.loc[(r61["analysis"] == "broad_52_132") & (r61["SoC_band"] == "90-100")]
        ng, g95 = high.loc[high["method"] == "no_guard"], high.loc[high["method"] == "guard_95"]
        if len(ng) and len(g95):
            a, b = float(ng.iloc[0]["mean_MAE_mV"]), float(g95.iloc[0]["mean_MAE_mV"])
            record("R6_no_guard_vs_guard95", "PASS" if a < b else "FAIL",
